// dunyo yarim o'lchami (m) → 5.2 km × 5.2 km\n W.SEA = 0; // dengiz sathi\n W.JUMPS = []; // {x,z,ry,len,gap,land,h,wid}: relyefga kiritilgan sakrash rampalari\n W.ZONES = [\n // Skyline Pass serpantini: o'q (0.78,0.62), 200 m ko'tarilish\n { x: -1120, z: -985, ax: 0.78, az: 0.62, ru: 560, rv: 420, y0: 232, slope: 0.21 },\n ];\n const clamp = (v, a, b) => v < a ? a : v > b ? b : v;\n const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + (b - a) * t;\n const sstep = (a, b, x) => { const t = clamp((x - a) / (b - a), 0, 1); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); };\n W.clamp = clamp; W.lerp = lerp; W.sstep = sstep;\n\n // ── Tasodif ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n W.rng = function (seed) {\n let a = seed >>> 0;\n return function () {\n a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) >>> 0;\n let t = a; t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);\n t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);\n return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;\n };\n };\n W.hash2 = function (x, z, s) {\n let h = (Math.imul(x | 0, 374761393) + Math.imul(z | 0, 668265263) + Math.imul((s || 0) | 0, 1442695041)) | 0;\n h = Math.imul(h ^ (h >>> 13), 1274126177); i < 256; i++) { const a = r() * 6.283185307; GX[i] = Math.cos(a); GZ[i] = Math.sin(a); }\n })();\n function noise2(x, z) {\n const xi = Math.floor(x), zi = Math.floor(z);\n const xf = x - xi, zf = z - zi;\n const X = xi & 255, Z = zi & 255;\n const u = xf * xf * xf * (xf * (xf * 6 - 15) + 10), v = zf * zf * zf * (zf * (zf * 6 - 15) + 10);\n const a = PERM[PERM[X] + Z], b = PERM[PERM[X + 1] + Z], c = PERM[PERM[X] + Z + 1], d = PERM[PERM[X + 1] + Z + 1];\n const n00 = GX[a] * xf + GZ[a] * zf;\n const n10 = GX[b] * (xf - 1) + GZ[b] * zf;\n const n01 = GX[c] * xf + GZ[c] * (zf - 1);\n const n11 = GX[d] * (xf - 1) + GZ[d] * (zf - 1);\n return lerp(lerp(n00, n10, u), lerp(n01, n11, u), v) * 1.4142; // ≈ [-1, 1]\n }\n W.noise2 = noise2;\n function fbm(x, z, oct, lac, gain) {\n let a = 1, f = 1, s = 0, n = 0;\n for (let i = 0; i < oct; i++) { s += a * noise2(x * f + i * 17.3, z * f - i * 9.1); n += a; a *= gain; f *= lac; }\n return s / n;\n }\n function ridged(x, z, oct) {\n let a = 1, f = 1, s = 0, n = 0;\n for (let i = 0; i < oct; i++) {\n let r = 1 - Math.abs(noise2(x * f + 31.7 * i, z * f - 12.9 * i)); r *= r;\n s += r * a; n += a; a *= 0.5; f *= 2.05;\n }\n return s / n;\n }\n W.fbm = fbm; W.ridged = ridged;\n\n // ── Hududlar (4 ta) ────────────────────────────────────────\n // 0 VALLEY — Yashil vodiy (SW) 1 HIGHLANDS — Tog'lar (NW/N)\n // 2 MESA — Cho'l/kanyon (NE) 3 COAST — Sohil (SE)\n W.REGIONS = [\n { id: 0, name: 'Verdant Valley', cx: -1350, cz: 1250 },\n { id: 1, name: 'Highridge', cx: -1250, cz: -1350 },\n { id: 2, name: 'Redrock Mesa', cx: 1300, cz: -1250 },\n { id: 3, name: 'Saltcoast', cx: 1350, cz: 1450 },\n ];\n const _rw = [0, 0, 0, 0];\n // Hudud og'irliklari (yig'indisi = 1). Chegara shovqin bilan buriladi.\n function regionWeights(x, z, out) {\n out = out || _rw;\n const wx = x + 380 * noise2(x / 900 + 3.1, z / 900), wz = z + 380 * noise2(x / 900 - 7.7, z / 900 + 5.2);\n let sum = 0;\n for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {\n const R = W.REGIONS[i], dx = wx - R.cx, dz = wz - R.cz;\n const d2 = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);\n const w = Math.exp(-Math.pow(d2 / 1250, 2.6) * 2.2) + 1e-4;\n out[i] = w; sum += w;\n }\n for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) out[i] /= sum;\n return out;\n }\n W.regionWeights = regionWeights;\n W.regionAt = function (x, z) {\n const w = regionWeights(x, z, [0, 0, 0, 0]); let b = 0;\n for (let i = 1; i < 4; i++) if (w[i] > w[b]) b = i;\n return b;\n };\n\n // ── Tabiiy relyef (yo'ldan oldin) ──────────────────────────\n const _w = [0, 0, 0, 0];\n function natH(x, z) {\n const w = regionWeights(x, z, _w);\n // VODIY — yumshoq tepaliklar\n const hV = 34 + 24 * fbm(x / 520, z / 520, 4, 2.0, 0.5) + 6 * fbm(x / 110, z / 110, 2, 2, 0.5);\n // TOG'LAR — qirrali cho'qqilar\n const rg = ridged(x / 980, z / 980, 5);\n const hH = 70 + 330 * rg * (0.55 + 0.45 * sstep(-0.4, 0.5, fbm(x / 1500 + 9, z / 1500, 2, 2, 0.5))) + 14 * fbm(x / 90, z / 90, 3, 2, 0.5);\n // MESA — tekis plato + zinapoyali tepaliklar\n const m0 = fbm(x / 700 + 40, z / 700 - 12, 3, 2, 0.5);\n const terr = Math.floor((m0 * 0.5 + 0.5) * 5) / 5, fr = ((m0 * 0.5 + 0.5) * 5) % 1;\n const stepped = terr + sstep(0.45, 1.0, fr) / 5;\n const hM = 62 + 78 * stepped + 3.2 * fbm(x / 60, z / 60, 3, 2, 0.5);\n // SOHIL — dengizga tushuvchi qiyalik (janub-sharq)\n const sx = (x * 0.62 + z * 0.78) - 1750 + 160 * noise2(x / 600, z / 600); i < P.length; i++) cum.push(cum[i - 1] + Math.hypot(P[i][0] - P[i - 1][0], P[i][1] - P[i - 1][1]));\n const total = cum[cum.length - 1];\n const n = closed ? {\n dense = [];\n const hp = def.segs.map(sg => sg.hairpins ? hairpins(sg.hairpins.x, sg.hairpins.z, sg.hairpins.ax, sg.hairpins.az, sg.hairpins.legs, sg.hairpins.legLen, sg.hairpins.spacing) : P.length, cl = !!def.closed, g = i => cl ? P[((i % n) + n) % n] : P[clamp(i, 0, n - 1)];\n for (let it = 0; it < 40; it++) {\n const Q2 = P.map(p => p.slice());\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n if (!cl && (i < 6 || i > n - 7)) continue;\n const a = g(i - 4), b = g(i), c = g(i + 4);\n const ab = Math.hypot(b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]), bc = Math.hypot(c[0] - b[0], c[1] - b[1]), ac = Math.hypot(c[0] - a[0], c[1] - a[1]);\n const area2 = Math.abs((b[0] - a[0]) * (c[1] - a[1]) - (b[1] - a[1]) * (c[0] - a[0]));\n const r = area2 > 1e-6 ? (ab * bc * ac) / (2 * area2) : 1e9;\n if (r < 22) { const m0 = (g(i - 2)[0] + g(i + 2)[0]) / 2, m1 = (g(i - 2)[1] + g(i + 2)[1]) / 2; Q2[i][0] = lerp(b[0], m0, 0.45); Q2[i][1] = lerp(b[1], m1, 0.45); }\n }\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) P[i] = Q2[i];\n }\n }\n return P;\n }\n\n function finishRoad(def, P, idx) {\n const n = P.length, closed = !!def.closed;\n const R = { id: def.id, name: def.name, idx, closed, n, def,\n x: new Float64Array(n), z: new Float64Array(n), y: new Float64Array(n),\n tx: new Float64Array(n), tz: new Float64Array(n), hw: new Float32Array(n),\n bank: new Float32Array(n), curv: new Float32Array(n), s: new Float64Array(n),\n surf: new Uint8Array(n), cls: new Uint8Array(n), flag: new Uint8Array(n), reg: new Uint8Array(n) };\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { R.x[i] = P[i][0]; R.z[i] = P[i][1]; R.s[i] = i * DS; }\n { let L = 0; const m = closed ? n : n - 1; for (let i = 0; i < m; i++) { const j = (i + 1) % n; L += Math.hypot(R.x[j] - R.x[i], R.z[j] - R.z[i]); } R.length = L; R.sp = L / (closed ? n : n - 1); }\n const nb = (i, d) => closed ? ((i + d) % n + n) % n : clamp(i + d, 0, n - 1);\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n const a = nb(i, -1), b = nb(i, 1);\n let tx = R.x[b] - R.x[a], tz = R.z[b] - R.z[a]; const L = Math.hypot(tx, tz) || 1;\n R.tx[i] = tx / L; R.tz[i] = tz / L;\n }\n // Sinf (waypointdan keyin) — silliq kenglik\n const clsAt = new Float64Array(n);\n if (def.pts && closed && def.pts[0].length > 2) {\n // loop: har waypointga sinf; yo'l uzunligi bo'yicha eng yaqin waypoint\n const wp = def.pts, cum = []; let tot = 0;\n for (let i = 0; i < wp.length; i++) { cum.push(tot); const q = wp[(i + 1) % wp.length]; tot += Math.hypot(q[0] - wp[i][0], q[1] - wp[i][1]); }\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n let best = 0, bd = 1e12;\n for (let k = 0; k < wp.length; k++) { const d = Math.hypot(R.x[i] - wp[k][0], R.z[i] - wp[k][1]); if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = k; } }\n clsAt[i] = wp[best][2];\n }\n } else clsAt.fill(def.cls || 1);\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n const c = W.CLS[clsAt[i]]; R.cls[i] = clsAt[i]; R.surf[i] = c.surf;\n R.hw[i] = c.hw; R.reg[i] = W.regionAt(R.x[i], R.z[i]);\n }\n // kenglikni 40 m ichida silliqlash\n for (let pass = 0; pass < 6; pass++) {\n const t = new Float32Array(n);\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { let s = 0, c = 0; for (let k = -10; k <= 10; k++) { s += R.hw[nb(i, k)]; c++; } t[i] = s / c; }\n R.hw.set(t);\n }\n // Egrilik (o'ngga burilish musbat)\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n const a = nb(i, -3), b = nb(i, 3);\n const rx = -R.tz[i], rz = R.tx[i];\n R.curv[i] = ((R.tx[b] - R.tx[a]) * rx + (R.tz[b] - R.tz[a]) * rz) / (6 * DS);\n }\n return R;\n }\n\n function computeHeights(R, anchors) {\n const n = R.n, closed = R.closed;\n const nb = (i, d) => closed ? ((i + d) % n + n) % n : clamp(i + d, 0, n - 1);\n let y = new Float64Array(n);\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) y[i] = natH(R.x[i], R.z[i]);\n const win = R.id === 'pass' ? 28 : 55;\n for (let pass = 0; pass < 3; pass++) {\n const t = new Float64Array(n);\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { let s = 0, c = 0; for (let k = -win; k <= win; k += 2) { s += y[nb(i, k)]; c++; } t[i] = s / c; }\n y = t;\n }\n // Ankerlar: boshqa yo'lga ulanish balandligi (farq katta bo'lsa uzoqroq silliq moslashish)\n const a0 = anchors ? anchors.start : null, a1 = anchors ? anchors.end : null;\n if (a0 != null || a1 != null) {\n const taper = (idx0, dir, target) => {\n const delta = target - y[idx0], T = Math.min(n - 1, Math.round(clamp(Math.abs(delta) / 0.05, 260, 2600) / DS));\n for (let k = 0; k < T; k++) { const i = idx0 + dir * k; if (i < 0 || i >= n) break; y[i] += delta * (1 - sstep(0, T, k)); }\n };\n if (a0 != null) taper(0, 1, a0); if (a1 != null) taper(n - 1, -1, a1);\n }\n // Qiyalik chegarasi (maks 8.5%, serpantinda 9.5%) — ankerlar qat'iy\n const g = (R.id === 'pass' ? 0.095 : 0.085) * DS;\n for (let it = 0; it < 6; it++) {\n const m = closed ? n * 2 : n;\n if (a0 != null && !closed) y[0] = a0; if (a1 != null && !closed) y[n - 1] = a1;\n for (let k = 1; k < m; k++) { const i = k % n, p = (k - 1 + n) % n; if (!closed && a1 != null && i === n - 1) continue; y[i] = clamp(y[i], y[p] - g, y[p] + g); }\n for (let k = m - 2; k >= 0; k--) { const i = k % n, p = (k + 1) % n; if (!closed && a0 != null && i === 0) continue; y[i] = clamp(y[i], y[p] - g, y[p] + g); }\n }\n if (a0 != null && !closed) y[0] = a0; if (a1 != null && !closed) y[n - 1] = a1;\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) R.y[i] = Math.max(y[i], W.SEA + 2.6);\n // Bank (yo'l qiyaligi): burilish ichiga\n const bk = new Float32Array(n);\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) bk[i] = clamp(R.curv[i] * 38, -0.075, 0.075);\n for (let pass = 0; pass < 4; pass++) { const t = new Float32Array(n); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { let s = 0; for (let k = -4; k <= 4; k++) s += bk[nb(i, k)]; t[i] = s / 9; } bk.set(t); }\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) R.bank[i] = R.surf[i] === 0 ? bk[i] : bk[i] * 0.5;\n }\n\n function flagStructures(R) {\n const n = R.n;\n const dh = new Float64Array(n);\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) dh[i] = R.y[i] - natH(R.x[i], R.z[i]); // + : ko'tarma / ko'prik\n const runs = (pred, minLen, grow, flag) => {\n let i = 0;\n while (i < n) {\n if (pred(i)) {\n let j = i; while (j < n && pred(j)) j++;\n if ((j - i) * DS >= minLen) {\n for (let k = Math.max(0, i - grow); k < Math.min(n, j + grow); k++) R.flag[k] |= flag;\n }\n i = j;\n } else i++;\n }\n };\n runs(k => dh[k] > 9.5, 44, 4, F_BRIDGE);\n // Tunnel: faqat tog'li/mesa hududlarida, chuqur kesma\n runs(k => dh[k] < -15 && (R.reg[k] === 1 || R.reg[k] === 2), 140, 10, F_TUNNEL);\n }\n\n // ── Daryo ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n function buildRiver() {\n let P = resample(splineDense(RIVER_PTS, false), false);\n const n = P.length, x = new Float64Array(n), z = new Float64Array(n); let y = new Float64Array(n);\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { x[i] = P[i][0] + 40 * noise2(i * DS / 300, 5.5); z[i] = P[i][1] + 40 * noise2(i * DS / 300, 9.1); y[i] = natH(x[i], z[i]); }\n for (let pass = 0; pass < 4; pass++) { const t = new Float64Array(n); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { let s = 0, c = 0; for (let k = -40; k <= 40; k += 2) { s += y[clamp(i + k, 0, n - 1)]; c++; } t[i] = s / c; } y = t; }\n let m = 1e9; const wy = new Float64Array(n);\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { m = Math.min(m, y[i] - 3.2); wy[i] = Math.max(m, 1.4); }\n // sohilga yetganda dengiz sathiga tushsin\n const tx = new Float64Array(n), tz = new Float64Array(n);\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const a = clamp(i - 1, 0, n - 1), b = clamp(i + 1, 0, n - 1); const dx = x[b] - x[a], dz = z[b] - z[a], L = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1; tx[i] = dx / L; tz[i] = dz / L; }\n W.river = { n, x, z, y: wy, tx, tz, hw: RIVER_HW };\n }\n // eng yaqin daryo namunasi (kichik: 200 ta atrofida — to'g'ridan-to'g'ri qidiruv, katak bilan)\n const RG = new Map();\n function indexRiver() {\n const r = W.river; RG.clear();\n for (let i = 0; i < r.n; i++) { const k = Math.floor(r.x[i] / CELL) * 100003 + Math.floor(r.z[i] / CELL); let a = RG.get(k); if (!a) RG.set(k, a = []); a.push(i); }\n }\n const RV = { d: 1e9, y: 0, i: -1 };\n function nearestRiver(x, z) {\n const r = W.river, cx = Math.floor(x / CELL), cz = Math.floor(z / CELL);\n let bd = 1e9, bi = -1;\n for (let a = -1; a <= 1; a++) for (let b = -1; b <= 1; b++) {\n const arr = RG.get((cx + a) * 100003 + cz + b); if (!arr) continue;\n for (let k = 0; k < arr.length; k++) { const i = arr[k], dx = x - r.x[i], dz = z - r.z[i], d = dx * dx + dz * dz; if (d < bd) { bd = d; bi = i; } }\n }\n RV.d = Math.sqrt(bd); RV.i = bi; RV.y = bi >= 0 ? r.y[bi] : 0;\n return bi >= 0;\n }\n\n // ── Fazoviy indeks (yo'llar) ──────────────────────────────\n function indexRoads() {\n GRID = new Map();\n for (const R of W.roads) for (let i = 0; i < R.n; i += 2) {\n const k = Math.floor(R.x[i] / CELL) * 100003 + Math.floor(R.z[i] / CELL);\n let a = GRID.get(k); if (!a) GRID.set(k, a = []);\n a.push(R.idx * 4194304 + i);\n }\n }\n // Natija (qayta ishlatiladigan struktura)\n const mkQ = () => ({ found: false, road: -1, i: -1, d: 1e9, l: 0, t: 0, y: 0, hw: 0, tanB: 0, flag: 0, cls: 0, surf: 0 });\n const Q = mkQ(); W.Q = Q;\n const CL = [mkQ(), mkQ(), mkQ()]; let CLn = 0;\n const cb = [{ d: 0, ri: -1, i: 0, key: -1 }, { d: 0, ri: -1, i: 0, key: -1 }, { d: 0, ri: -1, i: 0, key: -1 }];\n // namunadan segmentga proyeksiya: o'ngga musbat lateral, bank/kenglik interpolyatsiyasi\n function project(o, R, bi, x, z) {\n const n = R.n, nb = d => R.closed ? ((bi + d) % n + n) % n : clamp(bi + d, 0, n - 1);\n const tx = R.tx[bi], tz = R.tz[bi], dx = x - R.x[bi], dz = z - R.z[bi], t = dx * tx + dz * tz;\n const j = t > 0 ? nb(1) : nb(-1), frac = clamp(Math.abs(t) / DS, 0, 1);\n const rx = -tz, rz = tx;\n o.found = true; o.road = R.idx; o.i = bi; o.t = t;\n o.l = dx * rx + dz * rz; o.d = Math.abs(o.l);\n o.y = lerp(R.y[bi], R.y[j], frac); o.tanB = Math.tan(lerp(R.bank[bi], R.bank[j], frac)); o.hw = lerp(R.hw[bi], R.hw[j], frac);\n o.flag = R.flag[bi]; o.cls = R.cls[bi]; o.surf = R.surf[bi];\n }\n // eng yaqin (ko'pi bilan 3 ta) alohida yo'l bo'laklari: bir yo'lning uzoq qismlari (serpantin zinalari) ham alohida hisoblanadi\n function nearestClusters(x, z, maxD) {\n const cx = Math.floor(x / CELL), cz = Math.floor(z / CELL), rr = Math.ceil(maxD / CELL), lim = maxD * maxD;\n let m = 0;\n for (let a = -rr; a <= rr; a++) for (let b = -rr; b <= rr; b++) {\n const arr = GRID.get((cx + a) * 100003 + cz + b); if (!arr) continue;\n for (let k = 0; k < arr.length; k++) {\n const key = arr[k], ri = (key / 4194304) | 0, i = key - ri * 4194304, R = W.roads[ri];\n const dx = x - R.x[i], dz = z - R.z[i], d = dx * dx + dz * dz; if (d >= lim) continue;\n const ck = ri * 4096 + ((i / 90) | 0);\n let hit = -1; for (let q = 0; q < m; q++) if (cb[q].key === ck) { hit = q; break; }\n if (hit >= 0) { if (d < cb[hit].d) { cb[hit].d = d; cb[hit].i = i; } }\n else if (m < 3) { cb[m].d = d; cb[m].ri = ri; cb[m].i = i; cb[m].key = ck; m++; }\n else { let w = 0; for (let q = 1; q < 3; q++) if (cb[q].d > cb[w].d) w = q; if (d < cb[w].d) { cb[w].d = d; cb[w].ri = ri; cb[w].i = i; cb[w].key = ck; } }\n }\n }\n // saralash (yaqinidan uzoqqa)\n for (let a = 1; a < m; a++) { const t = { d: cb[a].d, ri: cb[a].ri, i: cb[a].i, key: cb[a].key }; let q = a - 1; while (q >= 0 && cb[q].d > t.d) { cb[q + 1].d = cb[q].d; cb[q + 1].ri = cb[q].ri; cb[q + 1].i = cb[q].i; cb[q + 1].key = cb[q].key; q--; } cb[q + 1].d = t.d; cb[q + 1].ri = t.ri; cb[q + 1].i = t.i; cb[q + 1].key = t.key; }\n for (let q = 0; q < m; q++) project(CL[q], W.roads[cb[q].ri], cb[q].i, x, z);\n CLn = m; return m;\n }\n function nearestRoad(x, z, maxD) {\n const m = nearestClusters(x, z, maxD);\n if (!m) { Q.found = false; return false; }\n const c = CL[0]; Q.found = true; Q.road = c.road; Q.i = c.i; Q.t = c.t; Q.l = c.l; Q.d = c.d; Q.y = c.y; Q.tanB = c.tanB; Q.hw = c.hw; Q.flag = c.flag; Q.cls = c.cls; Q.surf = c.surf;\n return true;\n }\n W.nearestRoad = nearestRoad;\n W.riverDist = (x, z) => (W.river && nearestRiver(x, z)) ? RV.d : 1e9;\n\n // ── Relyef (yo'l + daryo bilan) ───────────────────────────\n const SHOULDER = 1.7;\n const INFO = { water: -1e9, riverD: 1e9, onRoad: false, lat: 1e9, roadY: 0, tunnel: false, bridge: false };\n W.INFO = INFO;\n function heightAt(x, z, ground) {\n let h = natH(x, z);\n // daryo\n INFO.water = -1e9; INFO.riverD = 1e9;\n if (W.river && nearestRiver(x, z)) {\n const d = RV.d;\n if (d < RIVER_HW + 22) {\n const bed = RV.y - 2.4 + 4.6 * Math.pow(Math.min(1, d / RIVER_HW), 2);\n const w = 1 - sstep(RIVER_HW * 0.9, RIVER_HW + 22, d);\n h = lerp(h, Math.min(bed, h), w);\n INFO.water = RV.y; INFO.riverD = d;\n }\n }\n // yo'l (ko'p klasterli silliq aralashtirish)\n INFO.onRoad = false; INFO.tunnel = false; INFO.bridge = false; INFO.lat = 1e9; INFO.surf = -1;\n if (nearestRoad(x, z, 78)) {\n const c = Q, hwT = c.hw + SHOULDER, lat = c.d;\n INFO.roadY = c.y; INFO.lat = lat; INFO.surf = c.surf; INFO.hw = c.hw;\n const isT = (c.flag & F_TUNNEL) !== 0, isB = (c.flag & F_BRIDGE) !== 0;\n INFO.tunnel = isT; INFO.bridge = isB;\n if (ground && lat <= c.hw + 0.6) { h = c.y - clamp(c.l, -c.hw, c.hw) * c.tanB; INFO.onRoad = true; }\n else if (!ground && lat <= hwT && !isB && !isT) { INFO.onRoad = true; h = blendRoads(h); }\n else h = blendRoads(h);\n }\n return h;\n }\n // Normallashtirilgan og'irlik bilan bir nechta yo'l koridorini relyefga aralashtirish\n function blendRoads(h) {\n let S = 0, acc = 0;\n for (let k = 0; k < CLn; k++) {\n const c = CL[k]; if (c.flag & (F_BRIDGE | F_TUNNEL)) continue;\n const hwT = c.hw + SHOULDER, l = clamp(c.l, -hwT, hwT), yt = c.y - l * c.tanB;\n const bw = 9 + Math.min(34, Math.abs(h - yt) * 1.5), w = 1 - sstep(hwT, hwT + bw, c.d);\n S += w; acc += w * yt;\n }\n if (S <= 0) return h;\n return S <= 1 ? h * (1 - S) + acc : acc / S;\n }\n function blendRoad(h, yt, lat, hwT) {\n const bw = 9 + Math.min(34, Math.abs(h - yt) * 1.5);\n const t = 1 - sstep(hwT, hwT + bw, lat);\n return lerp(h, yt, t);\n }\n W.heightAt = (x, z) => heightAt(x, z, false);\n W.groundAt = (x, z) => heightAt(x, z, true);\n\n // Yuza turi: 0 asfalt,1 tuproq,2 shag'al,3 o't,4 qum,5 qor/tosh,6 ho'l tuproq(daryo bo'yi), 7 suv\n W.SURF_NAMES = ['asphalt', 'dirt', 'gravel', 'grass', 'sand', 'rock', 'mud', 'water'];\n W.surfaceAt = function (x, z) {\n const h = heightAt(x, z, true);\n if (INFO.onRoad) return Q.surf;\n if (INFO.water > -1e8 && h < INFO.water + 0.2 && INFO.riverD < RIVER_HW) return 7;\n if (h < 1.1) return h < -0.2 ? 7 : 4;\n const w = W.regionWeights(x, z, [0, 0, 0, 0]);\n if (h > 235 && w[1] > 0.4) return 5;\n if (w[2] > 0.5) return 4;\n if (w[3] > 0.5 && h < 9) return 4;\n if (w[1] > 0.5 && h > 120) return 5;\n return 3;\n };\n\n // ── Qurish ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n W.build = function () {\n if (W.built) return W;\n buildRiver(); indexRiver();\n W.roads.length = 0;\n // 1) halqa (loop)\n const built = {};\n ROAD_DEFS.forEach((def, idx) => {\n const P = buildRoad(def);\n const R = finishRoad(def, P, idx);\n W.roads.push(R); built[def.id] = R;\n });\n // 2) balandliklar: avval loop, keyin tarmoqlar (ulanish balandligi bilan)\n computeHeights(built.loop, null);\n const nearestOn = (P, px, pz) => { let bd = 1e12, bi = 0; for (let i = 0; i < P.n; i++) { const d = (P.x[i] - px) ** 2 + (P.z[i] - pz) ** 2; if (d < bd) { bd = d; bi = i; } } return bi; };\n for (const R of W.roads) {\n if (R.id === 'loop') continue;\n const j = R.def.join || [], anc = {};\n // oldin x,z ni ota yo'lga snap (balandlik hisoblashdan oldin)\n [[0, 1, j[0]], [R.n - 1, -1, j[1]]].forEach(([i0, dir, pid]) => {\n if (!pid) return; const P = built[pid], bi = nearestOn(P, R.x[i0], R.z[i0]), dx = P.x[bi] - R.x[i0], dz = P.z[bi] - R.z[i0], T = 160 / DS;\n for (let k = 0; k < T; k++) { const i = i0 + dir * k; if (i < 0 || i >= R.n) break; const w = 1 - sstep(0, T, k); R.x[i] += dx * w; R.z[i] += dz * w; }\n });\n const n = R.n;\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const a = clamp(i - 1, 0, n - 1), b = clamp(i + 1, 0, n - 1); const tx = R.x[b] - R.x[a], tz = R.z[b] - R.z[a], Ln = Math.hypot(tx, tz) || 1; R.tx[i] = tx / Ln; R.tz[i] = tz / Ln; }\n if (j[0]) anc.start = built[j[0]].y[nearestOn(built[j[0]], R.x[0], R.z[0])];\n if (j[1]) anc.end = built[j[1]].y[nearestOn(built[j[1]], R.x[n - 1], R.z[n - 1])];\n computeHeights(R, anc);\n }\n for (const R of W.roads) flagStructures(R);\n // ulanish nuqtalari atrofida (160 m) tunnel/ko'prik bo'lmasin\n const JP = [];\n for (const R of W.roads) { const j = R.def.join || []; if (j[0]) JP.push([R.x[0], R.z[0]]); if (j[1]) JP.push([R.x[R.n - 1], R.z[R.n - 1]]); }\n for (const R of W.roads) for (let i = 0; i < R.n; i++) { if (!R.flag[i]) continue; for (const q of JP) if ((R.x[i] - q[0]) ** 2 + (R.z[i] - q[1]) ** 2 < 160 * 160) { R.flag[i] = 0; break; } }\n indexRoads();\n // Statistika\n W.stats = { total: 0, byRoad: {} };\n for (const R of W.roads) { W.stats.byRoad[R.id] = +(R.length / 1000).toFixed(2); W.stats.total += R.length; }\n W.stats.total = +(W.stats.total / 1000).toFixed(2);\n // Sakrash rampalari: yo'l yonidagi tekis joylarga (relyef funksiyasiga kiritiladi)\n W.JUMPS.length = 0;\n [['hwy', 0.4, 1], ['hwy', 0.72, -1], ['canyon', 0.5, 1], ['link', 0.45, -1]].forEach(([id, f, side], k) => {\n const R = built[id], i = Math.floor(R.n * f), rx = -R.tz[i], rz = R.tx[i], off = R.hw[i] + 30;\n const x = R.x[i] + rx * side * off, z = R.z[i] + rz * side * off;\n // yo'l bo'ylab yo'nalish; rampa yo'lga parallel (yoki teskari)\n let ry = Math.atan2(R.tx[i], R.tz[i]); if (side < 0) ry += Math.PI;\n const y0 = natH(x, z);\n W.JUMPS.push({ id: 'jump' + k, name: 'Stunt Jump ' + (k + 1), x, z, ry, len: 20, gap: 24, land: 26, h: 3.6, wid: 10, y0 });\n });\n W.built = true;\n return W;\n };\n})(HZW);\n\n// ============================================================\n// APEX HORIZON — w3_mesh.js\n// Chunk mesher (sof): relyef, yo'l lentasi, bezaklar, inshootlar.\n// Natija — Float32Array/Uint32Array (Worker orqali uzatiladi).\n// ============================================================\n(function (W) {\n 'use strict';\n const { clamp, lerp, sstep, hash2, rng, fbm, noise2 } = W;\n W.CH = 128;\n const SH = 1.3; // yo'l chekkasi (shoulder)\n W.SHOULDER_W = SH;\n\n // ── Oddiy mesh quruvchi (yassi soyali, vertex rang) ────────\n function MB() { this.p = []; this.c = []; this.n = []; }\n MB.prototype.tri = function (a, b, c, col, j) {\n const ux = b[0] - a[0], uy = b[1] - a[1], uz = b[2] - a[2], vx = c[0] - a[0], vy = c[1] - a[1], vz = c[2] - a[2];\n let nx = uy * vz - uz * vy, ny = uz * vx - ux * vz, nz = ux * vy - uy * vx;\n const L = Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz) || 1; nx /= L; ny /= L; nz /= L;\n const k = 1 + (j || 0);\n for (const v of [a, b, c]) { this.p.push(v[0], v[1], v[2]); this.c.push(col[0] * k, col[1] * k, col[2] * k); this.n.push(nx, ny, nz); }\n };\n MB.prototype.quad = function (a, b, c, d, col, j) { this.tri(a, b, c, col, j); this.tri(a, c, d, col, j); };\n // Yo'naltirilgan quti: markaz, o'lcham, Y-burilish (heading), qo'shimcha pitch (x o'qi atrofida) yo'q\n MB.prototype.box = function (cx, cy, cz, sx, sy, sz, ry, col, j) {\n const c = Math.cos(ry || 0), s = Math.sin(ry || 0), hx = sx / 2, hy = sy / 2, hz = sz / 2;\n const P = (x, y, z) => [cx + x * c + z * s, cy + y, cz - x * s + z * c];\n const v = [P(-hx, -hy, -hz), P(hx, -hy, -hz), P(hx, hy, -hz), P(-hx, hy, -hz), P(-hx, -hy, hz), P(hx, -hy, hz), P(hx, hy, hz), P(-hx, hy, hz)];\n const jj = j || 0;\n this.quad(v[4], v[5], v[6], v[7], col, jj); this.quad(v[1], v[0], v[3], v[2], col, jj);\n this.quad(v[0], v[4], v[7], v[3], col, jj * 0.5); this.quad(v[5], v[1], v[2], v[6], col, jj * 0.5);\n this.quad(v[3], v[7], v[6], v[2], col, jj + 0.08); this.quad(v[0], v[1], v[5], v[4], col, -0.2);\n };\n // Piramida/konus (ko'p qirrali): asos markazi (cx,cy,cz), radius r, balandlik h, sides\n MB.prototype.cone = function (cx, cy, cz, r, h, sides, col, j, r2) {\n const top = [cx, cy + h, cz], t = r2 || 0;\n for (let i = 0; i < sides; i++) {\n const a0 = i / sides * 6.2832, a1 = (i + 1) / sides * 6.2832;\n const b0 = [cx + Math.cos(a0) * r, cy, cz + Math.sin(a0) * r], b1 = [cx + Math.cos(a1) * r, cy, cz + Math.sin(a1) * r];\n if (t > 0) {\n const t0 = [cx + Math.cos(a0) * t, cy + h, cz + Math.sin(a0) * t], t1 = [cx + Math.cos(a1) * t, cy + h, cz + Math.sin(a1) * t];\n this.quad(b1, b0, t0, t1, col, j);\n } else this.tri(b1, b0, top, col, j);\n }\n };\n // Elipsoid (past poligonli shar): ikosfera o'rniga 2 halqali \"kristall\"\n MB.prototype.blob = function (cx, cy, cz, rx, ry, rz, col, rnd, j) {\n const seg = 6, rings = [[-1, 0], [-0.45, 0.88], [0.25, 1], [0.8, 0.62], [1, 0]];\n const pts = rings.map(([h, k], ri) => { const a = []; for (let i = 0; i < seg; i++) { const ang = i / seg * 6.2832 + ri * 0.4, rr = k * (0.85 + 0.3 * rnd()); a.push([cx + Math.cos(ang) * rx * rr, cy + h * ry, cz + Math.sin(ang) * rz * rr]); } return k === 0 ? [[cx, cy + h * ry, cz]] : a; });\n for (let r = 0; r < pts.length - 1; r++) {\n const A = pts[r], B = pts[r + 1];\n for (let i = 0; i < seg; i++) {\n const i2 = (i + 1) % seg;\n if (A.length === 1) this.tri(A[0], B[i2], B[i], col, j);\n else if (B.length === 1) this.tri(A[i], A[i2], B[0], col, j);\n else this.quad(A[i], A[i2], B[i2], B[i], col, j);\n }\n }\n };\n W.MB = MB;\n const mix = (a, b, t) => [lerp(a[0], b[0], t), lerp(a[1], b[1], t), lerp(a[2], b[2], t)];\n W.mix = mix;\n\n // ── Relyef rangi ───────────────────────────────────────────\n const C = {\n grassA: [0.27, 0.46, 0.15], grassB: [0.36, 0.56, 0.2], field: [0.62, 0.55, 0.22], field2: [0.33, 0.52, 0.17],\n rock: [0.46, 0.43, 0.41], rockD: [0.32, 0.3, 0.3], snow: [0.93, 0.95, 0.98], alpine: [0.3, 0.4, 0.24],\n sandR: [0.74, 0.42, 0.25], sandR2: [0.62, 0.32, 0.2], sandY: [0.83, 0.7, 0.45], beach: [0.88, 0.8, 0.6], dryG: [0.46, 0.5, 0.25],\n mud: [0.3, 0.24, 0.16], dirtRoad: [0.5, 0.38, 0.25], grav: [0.58, 0.55, 0.5], asph: [0.12, 0.12, 0.13],\n };\n W.PAL = C;\n const _w = [0, 0, 0, 0];\n function terrainColor(x, z, h, slope, out) {\n const w = W.regionWeights(x, z, _w);\n const n = fbm(x / 60, z / 60, 2, 2, 0.5), n2 = noise2(x / 9, z / 9);\n // vodiy\n const fld = noise2(x / 150 + 7, z / 150) , stripe = Math.sin((x * 0.8 + z * 0.35) / 7) * 0.5 + 0.5;\n let cv = mix(C.grassA, C.grassB, clamp(n * 1.6 + 0.5, 0, 1));\n if (fld > 0.18) cv = mix(cv, mix(C.field, C.field2, stripe), 0.85);\n // tog'\n let ch = mix(C.alpine, C.rock, sstep(0.25, 0.6, slope) + sstep(150, 230, h) * 0.5);\n ch = mix(ch, C.rockD, sstep(0.55, 0.9, slope));\n ch = mix(ch, C.snow, sstep(238, 262, h + n * 18) * (1 - sstep(0.62, 0.95, slope) * 0.6));\n // mesa\n const band = Math.sin(h * 0.42 + n * 2.0) * 0.5 + 0.5;\n let cm = mix(C.sandR, C.sandR2, band); cm = mix(cm, C.sandY, sstep(0.3, 0.0, slope) * 0.5 * (n * 0.5 + 0.5));\n cm = mix(cm, C.rockD, sstep(0.6, 1.0, slope) * 0.4);\n // sohil\n let cc = mix(C.dryG, C.grassB, clamp(n + 0.5, 0, 1));\n cc = mix(cc, C.beach, 1 - sstep(2.5, 9, h)); cc = mix(cc, C.rock, sstep(0.55, 0.85, slope));\n for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) out[i] = cv[i] * w[0] + ch[i] * w[1] + cm[i] * w[2] + cc[i] * w[3];\n const k = 0.94 + 0.12 * n2; out[0] *= k; out[1] *= k; out[2] *= k;\n if (h < 0.4) { out[0] = lerp(out[0], 0.5, 0.5); out[1] = lerp(out[1], 0.45, 0.5); out[2] = lerp(out[2], 0.32, 0.5); }\n return out;\n }\n W.terrainColor = terrainColor;\n\n // ── Relyef tekstura qatlamlari (6 ta): o't, tosh, qor, qum, qizil qum, tuproq ──\n const _w3 = [0, 0, 0, 0];\n function splat(x, z, h, slope, lat, sf, out, o) {\n const w = W.regionWeights(x, z, _w3), n = fbm(x / 60, z / 60, 2, 2, 0.5);\n const rockS = sstep(0.28, 0.62, slope), rock = Math.max(rockS, w[1] * sstep(150, 230, h) * 0.55);\n const snow = w[1] * sstep(238, 262, h + n * 18) * (1 - sstep(0.62, 0.95, slope) * 0.6);\n const beach = Math.min(1, w[3] * (1 - sstep(2.5, 9, h)) + (h < 1.2 ? 1 : 0));\n let grass = w[0] + w[1] * (1 - sstep(120, 200, h)) + w[3] * (1 - beach), red = w[2] * (1 - rockS * 0.5), sand = beach + w[2] * 0.12;\n let dirt = lat < 40 ? (1 - sstep(0, 5.5, lat - 4)) * (sf === 0 ? 0.3 : 0.85) : 0;\n grass *= 1 - rock; red *= 1 - rock * 0.6; sand *= 1 - rock * 0.5;\n const sn = Math.min(1, snow * 1.2), tot = grass + rock + sn + sand + red + dirt + 1e-4;\n out[o] = grass / tot * 255; out[o + 1] = rock / tot * 255; out[o + 2] = sn / tot * 255; out[o + 3] = sand / tot * 255;\n out[o + 4] = red / tot * 255; out[o + 5] = dirt / tot * 255; out[o + 6] = 0; out[o + 7] = 0;\n }\n // ── Chunk qurish ───────────────────────────────────────────\n // lod: 0 → 4 m, 1 → 8 m, 2 → 16 m\n W.buildChunk = function (cx, cz, lod) {\n W.build();\n const CH = W.CH, x0 = cx * CH, z0 = cz * CH;\n const N = [32, 16, 8][lod], cell = CH / N;\n const G = N + 3, hg = new Float32Array(G * G);\n const latg = new Float32Array(G * G), surfg = new Int8Array(G * G);\n for (let j = 0; j < G; j++) for (let i = 0; i < G; i++) {\n const x = x0 + (i - 1) * cell, z = z0 + (j - 1) * cell;\n hg[j * G + i] = W.heightAt(x, z);\n latg[j * G + i] = W.INFO.lat; surfg[j * G + i] = W.INFO.surf;\n }\n // — yo'l ostini tushirish: mesh uchburchagi ichida relyef yo'l lentasidan baland bo'lib qolmasin\n // (kengaytirilgan to'r: chetdagi qo'shni katak ham hisoblanadi → chunklar orasida yoriq bo'lmaydi)\n const low = new Float32Array(G * G);\n {\n const lats = [-1, -0.5, 0, 0.5, 1], mrg = cell * 2.5;\n for (const R of W.roads) {\n const n = R.n;\n for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n if (R.flag[i]) continue;\n const px = R.x[i], pz = R.z[i];\n if (px < x0 - mrg || px >= x0 + CH + mrg || pz < z0 - mrg || pz >= z0 + CH + mrg) continue;\n const hw = R.hw[i], W2 = hw + SH, tb = Math.tan(R.bank[i]), rxn = -R.tz[i], rzn = R.tx[i];\n for (let li = 0; li < 7; li++) {\n const l = li < 5 ? lats[li] * hw : (li === 5 ? -W2 : W2), ll = clamp(l, -hw, hw);\n const x = px + rxn * l, z = pz + rzn * l, yR = R.y[i] - ll * tb + 0.03 - (Math.abs(l) > hw ? 0.05 : 0);\n const fx = (x - x0) / cell + 1, fz = (z - z0) / cell + 1, ci = Math.floor(fx), cj = Math.floor(fz);\n if (ci < 0 || cj < 0 || ci >= G - 1 || cj >= G - 1) continue;\n const tx = fx - ci, tz = fz - cj, a = cj * G + ci, b = a + 1, c = a + G, d = c + 1;\n let hI, v0, v1, v2;\n if (tx + tz <= 1) { v0 = a; v1 = b; v2 = c; hI = hg[a] + (hg[b] - hg[a]) * tx + (hg[c] - hg[a]) * tz; }\n else { v0 = d; v1 = c; v2 = b; hI = hg[d] + (hg[c] - hg[d]) * (1 - tx) + (hg[b] - hg[d]) * (1 - tz); }\n const ex = hI - yR;\n if (ex > 0.01) { if (ex > low[v0]) low[v0] = ex; if (ex > low[v1]) low[v1] = ex; if (ex > low[v2]) low[v2] = ex; }\n }\n }\n }\n }\n // — relyef (skirt bilan)\n const V = (N + 1) * (N + 1), skirtV = 4 * (N + 1), tot = V + skirtV;\n const pos = new Float32Array(tot * 3), nor = new Float32Array(tot * 3), col = new Float32Array(tot * 3), spl = new Uint8Array(tot * 8);\n const tmp = [0, 0, 0];\n let minY = 1e9, maxY = -1e9;\n for (let j = 0; j <= N; j++) for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) {\n const gi = (j + 1) * G + (i + 1), h = hg[gi], k = j * (N + 1) + i;\n const x = x0 + i * cell, z = z0 + j * cell;\n const dx = (hg[gi + 1] - hg[gi - 1]) / (2 * cell), dz = (hg[gi + G] - hg[gi - G]) / (2 * cell);\n const L = Math.hypot(dx, 1, dz);\n pos[k * 3] = x; pos[k * 3 + 1] = h - low[gi]; pos[k * 3 + 2] = z;\n nor[k * 3] = -dx / L; nor[k * 3 + 1] = 1 / L; nor[k * 3 + 2] = -dz / L;\n const slope = Math.min(1, Math.hypot(dx, dz));\n terrainColor(x, z, h, slope, tmp);\n if (W.JUMPS.length && W.jumpMask(x, z)) { tmp[0] = 0.55; tmp[1] = 0.55; tmp[2] = 0.52; }\n const lat = latg[gi];\n if (lat < 40) { // yo'l chekkasi/tuproq izi\n const sf = surfg[gi];\n const t = 1 - sstep(0, 5.5, lat - 4.0);\n if (t > 0) { const rc = sf === 0 ? C.grav : sf === 1 ? C.dirtRoad : C.grav; const m = mix([tmp[0], tmp[1], tmp[2]], rc, t * (sf === 0 ? 0.35 : 0.8)); tmp[0] = m[0]; tmp[1] = m[1]; tmp[2] = m[2]; }\n }\n col[k * 3] = tmp[0]; col[k * 3 + 1] = tmp[1]; col[k * 3 + 2] = tmp[2];\n splat(x, z, h, slope, lat, surfg[gi], spl, k * 8);\n if (h < minY) minY = h; if (h > maxY) maxY = h;\n }\n // skirt (chekka bo'ylab pastga)\n let sk = V;\n const edge = [];\n for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) edge.push([i, 0]);\n for (let j = 1; j <= N; j++) edge.push([N, j]);\n for (let i = N - 1; i >= 0; i--) edge.push([i, N]);\n for (let j = N - 1; j >= 1; j--) edge.push([0, j]);\n const skIdx = [];\n for (const [i, j] of edge) {\n const src = j * (N + 1) + i;\n pos[sk * 3] = pos[src * 3]; pos[sk * 3 + 1] = pos[src * 3 + 1] - 14; pos[sk * 3 + 2] = pos[src * 3 + 2];\n nor[sk * 3] = nor[src * 3]; nor[sk * 3 + 1] = nor[src * 3 + 1]; nor[sk * 3 + 2] = nor[src * 3 + 2];\n col[sk * 3] = col[src * 3]; col[sk * 3 + 1] = col[src * 3 + 1]; col[sk * 3 + 2] = col[src * 3 + 2];\n for (let q = 0; q < 8; q++) spl[sk * 8 + q] = spl[src * 8 + q];\n skIdx.push([src, sk]); sk++;\n }\n const idx = [];\n for (let j = 0; j < N; j++) for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {\n const a = j * (N + 1) + i, b = a + 1, c = a + (N + 1), d = c + 1;\n idx.push(a, c, b, b, c, d);\n }\n for (let k = 0; k < skIdx.length; k++) {\n const [s0, k0] = skIdx[k], [s1, k1] = skIdx[(k + 1) % skIdx.length];\n idx.push(s0, k0, s1, s1, k0, k1);\n }\n const out = { cx, cz, lod, minY: minY - 14, maxY, terrain: { pos, nor, col, spl, idx: new Uint32Array(idx) }, roads: [], water: null, props: null };\n\n // — yo'l lentalari (sinf bo'yicha) —\n const byCls = {};\n const nearChunk = (x, z, m) => x >= x0 - m && x < x0 + CH + m && z >= z0 - m && z < z0 + CH + m;\n for (const R of W.roads) {\n const n = R.n, last = R.closed ? n : n - 1;\n for (let i = 0; i < last; i += 1) {\n const j = (i + 1) % n;\n const mx = (R.x[i] + R.x[j]) / 2, mz = (R.z[i] + R.z[j]) / 2;\n if (!(mx >= x0 && mx < x0 + CH && mz >= z0 && mz < z0 + CH)) continue;\n let b = byCls[R.cls[i]]; if (!b) b = byCls[R.cls[i]] = { pos: [], nor: [], uv: [], idx: [], base: 0, ci: R.cls[i] };\n // lod2: yo'lni har 4-namunada (8 m) chizish uchun o'tkazib yuborish — murakkablik kamayadi\n if (lod === 2 && (i & 3) !== 0) continue;\n const step = lod === 2 ? 4 : 1, j2 = Math.min(last, i + step) % n;\n for (const q of [i, j2]) {\n const hw = R.hw[q], tb = Math.tan(R.bank[q]), rxn = -R.tz[q], rzn = R.tx[q];\n const W2 = hw + SH;\n const lats = [-W2, -hw, 0, hw, W2];\n for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) {\n const l = lats[k], ll = clamp(l, -hw, hw);\n const y = R.y[q] - ll * tb + 0.035 - (Math.abs(l) > hw ? 0.05 : 0);\n b.pos.push(R.x[q] + rxn * l, y, R.z[q] + rzn * l); b.nor.push(0, 1, 0);\n b.uv.push((l + W2) / (2 * W2), R.s[q] / 14);\n }\n }\n const o = b.base; b.base += 10;\n for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) { const a = o + k, bb = o + k + 1, c = o + 5 + k, d = o + 6 + k; b.idx.push(a, bb, c, bb, d, c); }\n // yo'nalish: normal yuqoriga bo'lishi kerak — keyingi tekshiruvda flip\n }\n }\n for (const k in byCls) {\n const b = byCls[k]; if (!b.idx.length) continue;\n // winding: birinchi uchburchak normali +Y bo'lsin\n const p = b.pos, i0 = b.idx[0], i1 = b.idx[1], i2 = b.idx[2];\n const ux = p[i1 * 3] - p[i0 * 3], uz = p[i1 * 3 + 2] - p[i0 * 3 + 2], vx = p[i2 * 3] - p[i0 * 3], vz = p[i2 * 3 + 2] - p[i0 * 3 + 2];\n if (uz * vx - ux * vz < 0) for (let t = 0; t < b.idx.length; t += 3) { const q = b.idx[t + 1]; b.idx[t + 1] = b.idx[t + 2]; b.idx[t + 2] = q; }\n out.roads.push({ ci: b.ci, pos: new Float32Array(b.pos), nor: new Float32Array(b.nor), uv: new Float32Array(b.uv), idx: new Uint32Array(b.idx) });\n }\n\n // — daryo —\n if (W.river) {\n const r = W.river, pp = [], ii = [], uvw = []; let base = 0;\n for (let i = 0; i < r.n - 1; i += 2) {\n if (!nearChunk(r.x[i], r.z[i], 30)) continue;\n const i2 = Math.min(r.n - 1, i + 2);\n for (const q of [i, i2]) { const nx = -r.tz[q], nz = r.tx[q], w = r.hw * 1.05; pp.push(r.x[q] + nx * w, r.y[q] - 0.25, r.z[q] + nz * w, r.x[q] - nx * w, r.y[q] - 0.25, r.z[q] - nz * w); uvw.push(q * W.DS / 9, 0, q * W.DS / 9, 1.6); }\n ii.push(base, base + 2, base + 1, base + 1, base + 2, base + 3); base += 4;\n }\n if (ii.length) out.water = { pos: new Float32Array(pp), uv: new Float32Array(uvw), idx: new Uint32Array(ii) };\n }\n\n // — bezaklar (faqat lod ≤ 1) —\n if (lod <= 1 && W.buildProps) out.props = W.buildProps(cx, cz, lod, hg, G, cell);\n return out;\n };\n})(HZW);\n\n// ============================================================\n// APEX HORIZON — w4_props.js\n// Bezaklar: o'simlik, toshlar, yo'l jihozlari, ko'prik, tunnel,\n// aholi punktlari. };\n switch (P.kind) {\n case 'village':\n for (let k = -7; k <= 7; k++) for (const side of [-1, 1]) {\n if (r() < 0.25) continue;\n const di = k * 18 + ((r() * 10) | 0), a = at(di, side * (P.hw + 11 + r() * 5));\n const big = r() < 0.2;\n add({ t: 'house', x: a.x, z: a.z, y: a.y, ry: a.ry + (side > 0 ? 1.35 : 1; R.signs.push(Object.assign({ i, dir, l, cell, w: w * f, h: h * f, name: -1, back: SGN.gray, hgt: 2.4 }, o || {})); };\n const rightL = (i) => hwA(i) + 1.9;\n // 1) tezlik chegarasi (ikkala yo'nalish)\n const step = R.cls[0] >= 3 ? mb.quad(c2, t1, t0, b, c, -0.04);\n mb.tri(b, t0, a, c, -0.1); mb.tri(d2, t1, c2, c, -0.1);\n }\n function house(mb, o) {\n const wc = WALLS[o.wall % WALLS.length], rc = ROOFS[o.roof % ROOFS.length];\n mb.box(o.x, o.y + o.h / 2 - 0.4, o.z, o.w, o.h + 0.8, o.d, o.ry, wc, 0.03);\n gable(mb, o.x, o.y + o.h - 0.4, o.z, o.w, o.d, 2.2 + o.w * 0.12, o.ry, rc);\n const c = Math.cos(o.ry), s = Math.sin(o.ry);\n mb.box(o.x + (o.w * 0.3) * c, o.y + o.h + 1.6, o.z - (o.w * 0.3) * s, 0.7, 2.2, 0.7, o.ry, CONC2); i < 6; i++) { const a = i / 6 * 6.28 + r(), ex = x + Math.cos(a) * 3.2 * sc, ez = z + Math.sin(a) * 3.2 * sc; mb.tri([x, y + 5 * sc, z], [ex, y + 4.2 * sc, ez], [x + Math.cos(a + 0.5) * 1.2 * sc, y + 5.3 * sc, z + Math.sin(a + 0.5) * 1.2 * sc], g, 0); mb.tri([x, y + 5 * sc, z], [x + Math.cos(a + 0.5) * 1.2 * sc, y + 5.3 * sc, z + Math.sin(a + 0.5) * 1.2 * sc], [ex, y + 4.2 * sc, ez], g, -0.1); }\n } else if (kind === 'cactus') {\n const g = col(0.24, 0.45, 0.25);\n mb.cone(x, y, z, 0.45 * sc, 3.2 * sc, 6, g, 0, 0.42 * sc); mb.box(p[0], 1.9, p[1], 2.2, 1, 3, o.ry, col(0.2, 0.4, 0.6)); }\n break; }\n }\n }\n\n\n // ══ MODEL KUTUBXONASI (LIB) ═════════════════════════════════\n // Biome (daraxt/buta/tosh), to'siq, bordyur, binolar — hammasi shablon.\n // Shablonlar GLB fayldan (models/...) yoki — fayl bo'lmasa — shu yerdagi standart quruvchilardan keladi.\n // Massiv formati: {pos,nor,col} (indekssiz uchburchaklar, vertex rang).\n W.STRUCT_TYPES = ['house', 'church', 'barn', 'silo', 'gas', 'diner', 'castle', 'view', 'turbine', 'light', 'pier'];\n W.STRUCT_FILE = { house: 'house', church: 'church', barn: 'barn', silo: 'silo', gas: 'gas_station', diner: 'diner', castle: 'castle', view: 'viewpoint', turbine: 'wind_turbine', light: 'lighthouse', pier: 'pier' };\n W.BIOME_FAMILIES = ['oak', 'pine', 'palm', 'cactus', 'bush', 'rock'];\n const toArr = mb => ({ pos: new Float32Array(mb.p), nor: new Float32Array(mb.n), col: new Float32Array(mb.c) });\n function bboxOf(A) { let a = 1e9, b = 1e9, c = 1e9, d = -1e9, e = -1e9, f = -1e9; for (let i = 0; i < A.pos.length; i += 3) { const x = A.pos[i], y = A.pos[i + 1], z = A.pos[i + 2]; if (x < a) a = x; if (y < b) b = y; if (z < c) c = z; if (x > d) d = x; if (y > e) e = y; if (z > f) f = z; } return [a, b, c, d, e, f]; }\n W.bboxOf = bboxOf;\n W.makeDefaultLib = function () {\n const L = { biome: {}, rail: [], curb: [], struct: {} };\n const seeds = { oak: 3, pine: 3, palm: 2, cactus: 2, bush: 3 };\n for (const k in seeds) { L.biome[k] = []; for (let v = 0; v < seeds[k]; v++) { const mb = new MB(); tree(mb, k, 0, 0, 0, 1, rng(1000 + v * 77 + k.length)); L.biome[k].push(toArr(mb)); } }\n L.biome.rock = []; [0, 1, 2, 0].forEach((reg, v) => { const mb = new MB(); mb.box(0, 0.45, 2, 0.18, 0.95, 0.18, 0, CONC2); L.rail.push(toArr(mb)); }\n { const mb = new MB(); mb.box(0, 0.09, 0, 0.3, 0.2, 4.0, 0, col(0.74, 0.74, 0.72), 0.02); L.curb.push(toArr(mb)); }\n // binolar\n const mk = (t, o, dims) => { const mb = new MB(); i < p.length; i += 3) {\n const px = p[i] * sx, py = p[i + 1] * sy, pz = p[i + 2] * sz;\n mb.p.push(x + px * c + pz * s, y + py, z - px * s + pz * c);\n mb.n.push(n[i] * c + n[i + 2] * s, n[i + 1], -n[i] * s + n[i + 2] * c);\n mb.c.push(cl[i] * t, cl[i + 1] * t, cl[i + 2] * t);\n }\n }\n W.appendModel = appendModel;\n\n // ── Asosiy chunk props quruvchi ────────────────────────────\n W.buildProps = function (cx, cz, lod, hg, G, cell) {\n initPOIs(); return [x + rxn * p[0] + R.tx[i] * dz, y + p[1], z + rzn * p[0] + R.tz[i] * dz]; };\n for (let k = 0; k < pts.length - 1; k++) { const cc2 = (i % 8 === 0 && k === 5) ? col(1, 0.93, 0.7) : ci; mb.quad(P(k, -seg / 2), P(k, seg / 2), P(k + 1, seg / 2), P(k + 1, -seg / 2), cc2, 0); mb.quad(P(k, -seg / 2), P(k + 1, -seg / 2), P(k + 1, seg / 2), P(k, seg / 2), cc2, 0); }\n // portallar\n const prev = R.flag[Math.max(0, i - st)] & W.F_TUNNEL, next = R.flag[Math.min(n - 1, i + st)] & W.F_TUNNEL;\n for (const [isEnd, dir] of [[!prev, -1], [!next, 1]]) if (isEnd) {\n for (const sg of [-1, 1]) { const p = side(sg * (w + 1.2)); mb.box(p[0] + R.tx[i] * dir * seg * 0.5, y + 5, p[1] + R.tz[i] * dir * seg * 0.5, 2.4, 10.5, 1.6, ry, cc); }\n mb.box(x + R.tx[i] * dir * seg * 0.5, y + 8.6, z + R.tz[i] * dir * seg * 0.5, (w + 2.4) * 2, 2.6, 1.6, ry, cc, 0.04);\n }\n }\n if (lod === 0 && R.urb && R.urb[i] && !(f & (W.F_BRIDGE | W.F_TUNNEL)) && R.surf[i] === 0) {\n const segL = Math.hypot(R.x[Math.min(n - 1, i + st)] - x, R.z[Math.min(n - 1, i + st)] - z) + 0.05;\n for (const sg of [-1, 1]) { const q = side(sg * (hw + SH - 0.15)), ye = y - sg * (hw + SH) * Math.tan(R.bank[i]) - 0.05; appendModel(mb, LIB.curb[0], q[0], ye, q[1], ry, 1, 1, segL / 4, 1); }\n }\n if (lod === 0 && !(f & W.F_BRIDGE) && !(f & W.F_TUNNEL)) {\n if (i % 8 === 0 && R.surf[i] === 0) for (const sg of [-1, 1]) { const p = side(sg * (hw + SH + 0.4)); 'oak' : pr3 < 0.8 ? 'pine' : 'bush';\n else if (reg === 1) kind = h > 215 ? null : (pr3 < 0.85 ? 'pine' : 'bush');\n else if (reg === 2) kind = pr3 < 0.7 ? 'cactus' : 'bush';\n else kind = h < 8 ? .94], [.8, .94], [1, .7]], cab: null, ex: ['openCockpit', 'roundLights', 'fenders', 'wireWheels'], hp: { mass: 850, kw: 60, vmax: 150, a0: 3.9, drive: 'rwd', grip: 0.78, brake: 7.6, steer: 0.95, down: 0.0, off: 0.3, gears: [3.4, 1.9, 1.2, 0.85] }, snd: { idle: 22, red: 105, type: 'vintage', rough: 0.5, turbo: false } }, ]; HZ.cars = { specs: SPECS, models: {} }; const GY = -0.6; // yer = −0.6 (markaz balandligi 0.6) // ── Loft ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── function interp(arr, f) { // arr: [[f, v...]] if (f <= arr[0][0]) return arr[0].slice(1); for (let i = 1; i < arr.length; i++) if (f <= arr[i][0]) { const a = arr[i - 1], b = arr[i], t = (f - a[0]) / (b[0] - a[0] || 1); return a.slice(1).map((v, k) => lerpN(v, b[k + 1], t)); } return arr[arr.length - 1].slice(1); } function loftGeo(secs, N, capEnds) { // secs: [{z, hw, yb, yt, n}] old(−z)→orqa(+z) const pos = [], idx = []; for (const s of secs) { const yc = (s.yb + s.yt) / 2, hh = (s.yt - s.yb) / 2; for (let k = 0; k < N; k++) { const a = k / N * Math.PI * 2, c = Math.cos(a), sn = Math.sin(a), e = 2 / s.n; pos.push(sg(c) * Math.pow(Math.abs(c), e) * s.hw, yc + sg(sn) * Math.pow(Math.abs(sn), e) * hh, s.z); } } for (let i = 0; i < secs.length - 1; i++) for (let k = 0; k < N; k++) { const a = i * N + k, b = i * N + (k + 1) % N, c = (i + 1) * N + k, d = (i + 1) * N + (k + 1) % N; idx.push(a, b, c, b, d, c); } if (capEnds) for (const [si, flip] of [[0, true], [secs.length - 1, false]]) { const s = secs[si], ci = pos.length / 3; pos.push(0, (s.yb + s.yt) / 2, s.z); for (let k = 0; k < N; k++) { const a = si * N + k, b = si * N + (k + 1) % N; flip ? idx.push(ci, b, a) : idx.push(ci, a, b); } } const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos, 3)); g.setIndex(idx); g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; } function bodySections(sp) { const secs = [], M = 22; for (let i = 0; i <= M; i++) { const f = i / M, [top] = interp(sp.top, f), [wf] = interp(sp.wid, f); const nose = f < 0.05 ? (0.05 - f) / 0.05 : 0, tail = f > 0.95 ? (f - 0.95) / 0.05 : 0; secs.push({ z: -sp.L / 2 + f * sp.L, hw: Math.max(0.12, wf * sp.W / 2), yb: sp.gc + 0.08 * nose + 0.05 * tail, yt: top, n: 3.2 + 1.4 * (1 - nose) }); } return secs; } function cabinSections(sp) { const pts = sp.cab, secs = [], M = 12, f0 = pts[0][0], f1 = pts[pts.length - 1][0]; for (let i = 0; i <= M; i++) { const f = lerpN(f0, f1, i / M), r = interp(sp.cab, f), top = interp(sp.top, f)[0]; const roof = Math.max(r[0], top + 0.02); secs.push({ z: -sp.L / 2 + f * sp.L, hw: r[1] * sp.W / 2, yb: top - 0.02, yt: roof, n: 3.0 }); } return secs; } const matCache = {}; function paintMat(color, finish) { const m = finish === 'matte' ? new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color, roughness: 0.78, metalness: 0.05 }) : finish === 'metallic' ? new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color, roughness: 0.32, metalness: 0.65, clearcoat: 0.6, clearcoatRoughness: 0.15 }) : new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color, roughness: 0.22, metalness: 0.25, clearcoat: 0.9, clearcoatRoughness: 0.08 }); m.name = 'Paint'; return m; } const dark = () => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x15161a, roughness: 0.7, metalness: 0.2 }); const glassM = () => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x111a22, roughness: 0.05, metalness: 0.85, emissive: 0x06101a, name: 'Glass' }); const chrome = () => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xd6d9de, roughness: 0.18, metalness: 0.95 }); function boxM(g, w, h, d, x, y, z, mat, ry) { const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d), mat); m.position.set(x, y, z); if (ry) m.rotation.y = ry; g.add(m); return m; } function buildWheel(sp, name, x, y, z, variant) { const g = new THREE.Group(); g.name = name; g.position.set(x, y, z); g.rotation.order = 'YXZ'; const tire = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(sp.wr, sp.wr, sp.ww, 22), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x141414, roughness: 0.92 })); tire.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2; g.add(tire); const wire = variant === 'wire'; const rim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(sp.wr * (wire ? 0.8 : 0.66), sp.wr * (wire ? 0.8 : 0.66), sp.ww + 0.02, 18), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: wire ? 0xb8bcc4 : 0xc4c8cf, roughness: 0.25, metalness: 0.9, wireframe: false })); rim.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2; if (wire) { rim.material.transparent = true; rim.material.opacity = 0.35; } g.add(rim); const sm = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xaeb2ba, roughness: 0.3, metalness: 0.9 }); const n = wire ? 12 : 5; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const s = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(sp.ww + 0.03, sp.wr * (wire ? 1.5 : 1.18), wire ? 0.012 : 0.05), sm); s.rotation.x = i / n * Math.PI * (wire ? 1 : 2) ; g.add(s); } const disc = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(sp.wr * 0.4, sp.wr * 0.4, sp.ww * 0.5, 14), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x555a60, roughness: 0.4, metalness: 0.8 })); disc.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2; g.add(disc); return g; } // ── Tashqi (GLB) modeldan qurish ─────────────────────────── // Fayl konventsiyasi: Y yuqoriga, mashina old tomoni +Z (glTF standarti). APEX old tomoni −Z → 180° buriladi. HZ.cars.fromTemplate = function (sp, tpl, opt) { opt = opt || {}; const inner = tpl.clone(true); inner.traverse(o => { if (o.isMesh) { o.material = Array.isArray(o.material) ? o.material.map(m => m.clone()) : o.material.clone(); o.castShadow = true; o.receiveShadow = false; } }); const G = new THREE.Group(); G.name = 'Car_' + sp.id; const front = new THREE.Group(); front.rotation.y = Math.PI; front.add(inner); G.add(front); G.updateMatrixWorld(true); let bb = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(G), sz = bb.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()); // ⚠ Internetdan olingan GLB fayllarda o'lcham birligi noto'g'ri bo'lishi odatiy hol (sm/dyuym/ // noto'g'ri eksport masshtabi): model 0.1–1 m kabi haqiqatdan kichik yoki 50+ m kabi katta chiqishi mumkin. // Shuning uchun HAR DOIM maqsadli uzunlikka (sp.L) moslab masshtablanadi — "custom" (do'kon) mashina ham bundan mustasno emas. const natZ = Math.max(1e-4, sz.z); let k = sp.L / natZ; if (!isFinite(k) || k <= 0) k = 1; else k = Math.min(400, Math.max(0.0025, k)); // mutlaq xavfsizlik chegarasi front.scale.setScalar(k); G.updateMatrixWorld(true); bb.setFromObject(G); front.position.set(-(bb.min.x + bb.max.x) / 2, GY - bb.min.y, -(bb.min.z + bb.max.z) / 2); G.updateMatrixWorld(true); bb.setFromObject(G); sz = bb.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()); // g'ildiraklar: nomi bo'yicha topiladi, joyi bo'yicha (old/orqa, chap/o'ng) tasniflanadi const cand = []; inner.traverse(o => { if (/wheel|tire|tyre/i.test(o.name || '') && !/steer/i.test(o.name || '')) cand.push(o); }); const top = cand.filter(o => { let p = o.parent; while (p && p !== inner) { if (cand.indexOf(p) >= 0) return false; p = p.parent; } return true; }); const clusters = []; for (const o of top) { const b = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(o), c = b.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3()); let cl = clusters.find(q => q.c.distanceTo(c) < 0.3); if (!cl) { cl = { c, nodes: [], r: 0 }; clusters.push(cl); } cl.nodes.push(o); cl.r = Math.max(cl.r, (b.max.y - b.min.y) / 2); } G.userData.wheels = {}; if (clusters.length === 4) { for (const cl of clusters) { const key = 'Wheel_' + (cl.c.z < 0 ? 'F' : 'R') + (cl.c.x < 0 ? 'L' : 'R'), pivot = new THREE.Group(); pivot.name = key; pivot.rotation.order = 'YXZ'; pivot.position.copy(cl.c); G.add(pivot); G.updateMatrixWorld(true); for (const n of cl.nodes) pivot.attach(n); G.userData.wheels[key] = pivot; if (sp.custom) sp.wr = cl.r || sp.wr; } } // bo'yoq / shisha / chiroq materiallari — nomi bo'yicha const paint = [], hl = [], tl = []; let glassN = 0; inner.traverse(o => { if (!o.isMesh) return; const ms = Array.isArray(o.material) ? o.material : [o.material]; for (const m of ms) { const nm = m.name || ''; if (/^paint|body|carpaint|exterior|karoser/i.test(nm)) { if (paint.indexOf(m) < 0) paint.push(m); } else if (/head.?light|head.?lamp|front.?light/i.test(nm)) hl.push(m); else if (/tail|brake|rear.?light/i.test(nm)) tl.push(m); else if (/glass|window|oyna/i.test(nm)) { o.name = 'Glass'; glassN++; } } }); for (const m of hl.concat(tl)) if (m.emissive && m.emissive.getHex() === 0) m.emissive.copy(m.color); G.userData.paintMats = paint; G.userData.paint = paint[0] || null; G.userData.hlMats = hl; G.userData.tlMats = tl; G.userData.hlMat = hl[0] || null; G.userData.tlMat = tl[0] || null; const ints = []; inner.traverse(o => { if (/^interior$/i.test(o.name || '')) ints.push(o); }); if (ints[0]) { ints[0].visible = false; G.userData.interior = ints[0]; } // faralar G.userData.lights = []; if (!opt.noLights) for (const sx of [-1, 1]) { const l = new THREE.SpotLight(0xfff0cc, 0, 95, 0.62, 0.7, 2); l.position.set(sx * sz.x * 0.28, bb.min.y + sz.y * 0.32, bb.min.z + 0.1); l.target.position.set(sx * sz.x * 0.17, bb.min.y - 0.2, bb.min.z - 24); l.name = 'Headlight'; G.add(l); G.add(l.target); G.userData.lights.push(l); } G.userData.spec = sp; G.userData.fromFile = true; G.userData.size = sz; return G; }; // tashqi model uchun spesifikatsiya (shop/ dagi yangi mashinalar) HZ.cars.registerExtra = function (name, tpl, cfg) { cfg = cfg || {}; const title = String(name).replace(/[_\-]+/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase()), id = String(name).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''); const g = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(tpl), sz0 = g.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()); // Maqsadli uzunlik (metrda): `cfg.length` — aniq uzunlik; `cfg.scale` — modelning tabiiy o'lchamiga ko'paytiruvchi. // Hech biri berilmasa va model o'lchami real mashinaga mos kelmasa (2.2–8.5 m oralig'idan tashqari), // noto'g'ri eksport birligi deb hisoblanadi va standart uzunlikka (4.3 m) tuzatiladi. let L = cfg.length || (cfg.scale ? sz0.z * cfg.scale : sz0.z); if (!cfg.length && !cfg.scale && !(L > 2.2 && L < 8.5)) L = 4.3; const k0 = L / Math.max(1e-4, sz0.z), W = cfg.width || sz0.x * k0, H = sz0.y * k0; const hpDef = { mass: 1500, kw: 200, vmax: 220, a0: 6.5, drive: 'rwd', grip: 0.9, brake: 10, steer: 1, down: 0.1, off: 0.4, gears: [3.4, 2.2, 1.55, 1.15, 0.9, 0.75] }; const hp = Object.assign(hpDef, cfg.hp || {}); for (const k of ['mass', 'kw', 'vmax', 'a0', 'drive', 'grip', 'brake', 'steer', 'down', 'off', 'gears']) if (cfg[k] !== undefined) hp[k] = cfg[k]; const sp = { id, name: cfg.name || title, type: cfg.type || 'Shop car', cls: cfg.cls || 'C', pi: cfg.pi || 500, color: cfg.color || '#cccccc', finish: 'gloss', custom: true, price: cfg.price || 40000, L, W, gc: 0.15, wr: cfg.wr || 0.33, ww: 0.22, top: [[0, H * 0.45], [0.3, H * 0.5], [0.6, H * 0.62], [1, H * 0.5]], wid: [[0, 0.8], [1, 0.8]], cab: [[0.3, 0, 0.8], [0.45, H, 0.7], [0.8, H, 0.7], [0.95, 0, 0.8]], ex: [], hp, snd: Object.assign({ idle: 24, red: 140, type: 'i4', rough: 0.3, turbo: false }, cfg.snd || {}) }; HZ.cars.specs.push(sp); HZ.assets.tpl['custom/' + id] = tpl; return sp; }; // Asosiy kirish: fayl bo'lsa u, bo'lmasa — kodda qurilgan standart HZ.cars.make = function (sp, opt) { const A = HZ.assets; let tpl = A && A.ready ? (sp.custom && A.tpl['custom/' + sp.id] || A.carTemplate(sp, opt && opt.shop)) : null; if (tpl) { try { return HZ.cars.fromTemplate(sp, tpl, opt); } catch (e) { HZ.log('Model xato (' + sp.name + '): ' + e.message, 'le'); } } return HZ.cars.build(sp); }; HZ.cars.build = function (sp) { const G = new THREE.Group(); G.name = 'Car_' + sp.id; const paint = paintMat(sp.color, sp.finish); G.userData.paint = paint; const body = new THREE.Mesh(loftGeo(bodySections(sp), 18, true), paint); body.name = 'Body'; body.castShadow = true; G.add(body); if (sp.cab) { const cab = new THREE.Mesh(loftGeo(cabinSections(sp), 14, true), glassM()); cab.name = 'Glass'; cab.castShadow = true; G.add(cab); const cs = cabinSections(sp), roofSecs = cs.slice(2, cs.length - 2).map(s => ({ z: s.z, hw: s.hw * 0.86, yb: s.yt - 0.05, yt: s.yt + 0.025, n: 3.4 })); const roof = new THREE.Mesh(loftGeo(roofSecs, 12, true), paint); roof.name = 'Roof'; G.add(roof); } const hw = sp.W / 2, zf = -sp.L / 2 + sp.L * sp.wf, zr = sp.L / 2 - sp.L * sp.wrf, wy = sp.wr; const trk = hw * 0.93 - sp.ww / 2 + 0.02, wv = sp.ex.includes('wireWheels') ? 'wire' : ''; const wh = [['Wheel_FL', -trk, zf], ['Wheel_FR', trk, zf], ['Wheel_RL', -trk, zr], ['Wheel_RR', trk, zr]]; G.userData.wheels = {}; for (const [nm, x, z] of wh) { const w = buildWheel(sp, nm, x, wy, z, wv); HZ.tune.powerMul || 1 : 1), 9.6 * mu * driveF); const P = h.kw * 1000 * 0.82 * (HZ.tune ? HZ.tune.powerMul || 1 : 1), vv = Math.max(v, 4); let a = Math.min(a0, P / (h.mass * vv)); a *= 1 - Math.pow(vk, 3.2); // aerodinamik cheklov a *= (D.shift > 0 ? 0.35 : 1); D.wheelSpin = 0; if (D.assists.tc === false && a0 > 9.6 * mu * driveF * 0.98 && v < 25) D.wheelSpin = 1; let gCap = 0; if (D.manual && !(HZ.auto && HZ.auto.on)) { const gr = h.gears[D.gearI], red = D.red || 7000, rpm = av2(v, sp.wr, gr), rn = (rpm - 850) / Math.max(1, red - 850); let tf = rpm < 1400 ? 0.22 + 0.5 * (rpm / 1400) : 1; if (rn > 0.93) tf = Math.max(0, 1 - (rn - 0.93) * 14); if (v < 4 && D.gearI <= 1) tf = Math.max(tf, 0.9); a *= tf; gCap = red * 0.985 * sp.wr * 2 * Math.PI / (60 * gr * 3.4); } cfg.accel = Math.max(0.05, a / 1.6); cfg.maxSpeed = h.vmax * (D.surf >= 3 && D.surf !== 5 ? lerp(0.62, 0.92, off) : D.surf === 1 || D.surf === 2 ? lerp(0.82, 0.98, off) : 1); if (gCap) cfg.maxSpeed = Math.min(cfg.maxSpeed, gCap * 3.6); cfg.brake = Math.max(2, (h.brake * (HZ.tune ? HZ.tune.brakeMul || 1 : 1) * clamp(mu, 0.3, 1.1) * (D.assists.abs ? 1 : 0.9)) / 1.5); cfg.friction = 0.5; cfg.grip = clamp(0.5 + 0.5 * h.grip * clamp(mu, 0.2, 1.05), 0.35, 0.99); cfg.driftAmount = 0.5; D.v0 = ud.speed; D.yaw0 = car.rotation.y; D.throttle = fpsKeys['KeyW'] ? 1 : 0; D.brakeOn = !!fpsKeys['KeyS'] && ud.speed > 0.5; D.g0 = W.groundAt(x, z); D.p0x = x; D.p0z = z; D.p0y = car.position.y; D.wasGrounded = car.position.y - D.g0 < 0.9; }; // ── Har kadrdan keyin ────────────────────────────────────── D.post = function (delta) { const car = HZ.player, ud = car.userData, cfg = window._getCarCfg(car), sp = D.spec, h = sp.hp; let v = ud.speed; // 0) ORQAGA YURISH: S bosilsa va mashina to'xtagan/orqaga ketayotgan bo'lsa — rear gear { const kS = !!fpsKeys['KeyS'], kW = !!fpsKeys['KeyW']; if (kS && !kW && D.v0 <= 0.6) { const acc = 5.4 * clamp(D.mu, 0.3, 1); ud.speed = v = Math.max(-12, D.v0 - acc * delta); } else if (kW && !kS && D.v0 < -0.4) { ud.speed = v = Math.min(0.2, D.v0 + 14 * delta); } } const av = Math.abs(v); // 1) bo'sh yurish: haqiqiy sekinlashuv (dvigatel tormozi + qarshilik) const throttle = !!fpsKeys['KeyW'], braking = !!fpsKeys['KeyS'] || !!fpsKeys['Space']; if (!throttle && !braking && D.v0 !== undefined) { const dec = (0.5 + 0.0009 * D.v0 * D.v0 + DRAG[D.surf] * 9.8 * 0.7 + 1.1 * Math.min(1, av / 3)) * (HZ.tune && HZ.tune.dragMul || 1); const base = D.v0 > 0 ? Math.max(0, D.v0 - dec * delta) : Math.min(0, D.v0 + dec * delta); ud.speed = v = base; } // 2) rul: ko'ndalang tezlanish chegarasi (understeer) const dYaw = car.rotation.y - D.yaw0; const downF = 1 + h.down * (av / 40) * (av / 40); let aLat = D.mu * 9.81 * (0.82 + 0.3 * h.grip) * downF * (HZ.tune && HZ.tune.gripMul || 1); if (fpsKeys['Space'] && av > 8) aLat *= 0.35; // qo'l tormozi: orqa yo'qotadi const wLim = aLat / Math.max(av, 4), maxD = wLim * delta; D.slip = 0; if (av > 3 && Math.abs(dYaw) > maxD && !fpsKeys['Space']) { const ex = Math.abs(dYaw) - maxD; car.rotation.y = D.yaw0 + Math.sign(dYaw) * maxD; D.slip = clamp(ex / (Math.abs(dYaw) + 1e-6), 0, 1); // ortiqcha burilish tezlikni olib ketadi (suzish) ud.speed *= 1 - D.slip * 0.35 * delta * 4; } // 3) to'siqlar: tik qiyalik, suv, dunyo chegarasi (rampadan uchish — to'siq emas) const x = car.position.x, z = car.position.z, g1 = W.groundAt(x, z); const step = Math.hypot(x - D.p0x, z - D.p0z) || 1e-6; const rise = (g1 - D.g0) / step, drop = (D.g0 - g1) / step; const grounded0 = D.p0y - (D.g0 + 0.6) < 0.12, ballY0 = D.p0y + (D.slopePrev || 0) * step; const takeoff = !D.fly && grounded0 && av > 10 && (g1 + 0.6) < ballY0 - 0.28 && (D.slopePrev || 0) > 0.05; const out = Math.max(Math.abs(x), Math.abs(z)) > W.HALF - 20; const water = g1 < -0.45 && !(W.INFO.onRoad); const wallRise = rise > 0.95 && (!D.fly || D.p0y < g1 + 0.2); const wallDrop = D.wasGrounded && drop > 0.95 && step > 0.05 && !takeoff && !D.fly; if (step > 0.02 && (wallRise || wallDrop || out || water)) { D.lastWall = { wallRise, wallDrop, out, water, rise: +rise.toFixed(2), drop: +drop.toFixed(2), x: x | 0, z: z | 0, g0: +D.g0.toFixed(1), g1: +g1.toFixed(1), step: +step.toFixed(2), onRoad: W.INFO.onRoad }; car.position.x = D.p0x; car.position.z = D.p0z; car.position.y = D.p0y; D.fly = false; D.impact = Math.min(1, Math.abs(ud.speed) / 30); ud.speed *= -0.18; ud.lateralVel = 0; HZ.fire && HZ.fire('impact', D.impact); } // 4) havoda: ballistik uchish (tepalik/rampadan sakrash) const gNow = g1 + 0.6, car_y0 = D.p0y; if (!D.fly) { if (takeoff) { D.fly = true; D.vy = (D.slopePrev || 0) * av; D.air = 0; } else if (av > 10 && (g1 + 0.6) < ballY0 - 0.28 && grounded0 && (D.slopePrev || 0) > -0.2 && step > 0.02) { D.fly = true; D.vy = (D.slopePrev || 0) * av; D.air = 0; } else D.slopePrev = (D.slopePrev || 0) * 0.6 + 0.4 * clamp((g1 - D.g0) / step, -1, 1); } if (D.fly) { D.vy -= 17 * delta; let ny = car_y0 + D.vy * delta; D.air += delta; if (ny <= gNow) { ny = gNow; D.fly = false; const vv = -D.vy; if (vv > 5) { D.impact = clamp(vv / 18, 0.1, 1); HZ.fire('impact', D.impact * 0.6); ud.speed *= clamp(1 - vv * 0.01, 0.8, 1); } D.landed = D.air; D.slopePrev = 0; } car.position.y = ny; if (HZ.model) HZ.model.rotation.x += ((-clamp(D.vy / Math.max(av, 8), -0.5, 0.5)) - HZ.model.rotation.x) * Math.min(1, delta * 4); } else D.air = 0; D.wasAir = D.fly; // 5) uzatma + RPM const gears = h.gears, fin = 3.4, wr = sp.wr; let gi = D.gearI; const rpmAt = (g) => av / wr * gears[g] * fin * 60 / (2 * Math.PI); const idle = 850, red = 5200 + sp.snd.red * 18 - 1500 + 1000; D.red = Math.min(8600, Math.max(6500, red)); const redline = D.red; if (v < -0.5) { D.gear = -1; gi = 0; } else if (D.manual && !(HZ.auto && HZ.auto.on)) { gi = D.gearI; D.gear = gi + 1; } else { if (rpmAt(gi) > redline * 0.92 && gi < gears.length - 1) { gi++; D.shift = 0.22; HZ.fire && HZ.fire('shift', 1); } else if (gi > 0 && rpmAt(gi - 1) < redline * 0.62) { gi--; D.shift = 0.18; } D.gear = gi + 1; } D.gearI = gi; D.shift = Math.max(0, D.shift - delta); const target = Math.max(idle, Math.min(redline, rpmAt(gi))); D.rpm += ((throttle && av < 2 ? target + 2500 * (HZ.nitroOn ? 1 : 1) : target) - D.rpm) * Math.min(1, delta * 14); if (D.rpm > redline) D.rpm = redline - (Math.random() * 120); D.kmh = Math.abs(ud.speed) * 3.6; D.speed = ud.speed; // 6) g'ildiraklar va kuzov const wh = HZ.model && HZ.model.userData.wheels; if (wh) { D.spin = (D.spin || 0) - ud.speed * delta / sp.wr * (D.wheelSpin ? 1.6 : 1); const st = clamp(ud.steer * 0.7, -0.5, 0.5); for (const k in wh) { const w = wh[k]; w.rotation.x = D.spin; if (k.indexOf('F') > 0) w.rotation.y = st; } } // lateral siljish — kuzov og'ishi (roll) const m = HZ.model; if (m) { const tgt = clamp(-(ud.steer || 0) * Math.min(1, av / 25) * 0.07 - (ud.lateralVel || 0) * 0.01, -0.12, 0.12); m.rotation.z += (tgt - m.rotation.z) * Math.min(1, delta * 6); const acc = (ud.speed - D.v0) / Math.max(delta, 1e-3); const pt = clamp(-acc * 0.004, -0.05, 0.05); m.rotation.x += (pt - m.rotation.x) * Math.min(1, delta * 5); // chiroqlar: stop-signal, faralar (L bilan: auto → yoq → o'ch) const lo = HZ.lightsOn(), U2 = m.userData; for (const tm of (U2.tlMats && U2.tlMats.length ? U2.tlMats : [U2.tlMat]).filter(Boolean)) tm.emissiveIntensity = (braking && av > 0.3) ? 2.4 : (lo ? 0.9 : 0.25); for (const hm of (U2.hlMats && U2.hlMats.length ? U2.hlMats : [U2.hlMat]).filter(Boolean)) hm.emissiveIntensity = lo ? 1.8 : 0.25; if (U2.lights) for (const l of U2.lights) l.intensity = lo ? 420 : 0; } // 7) drift hisoblash const lat = Math.abs(ud.lateralVel || 0); D.drift = av > 8 && (lat > 3 || D.slip > 0.25 || (fpsKeys['Space'] && av > 10 && Math.abs(ud.steer) > 0.1)) ? Math.min(1, (lat + D.slip * 8) / 12 + 0.25) : D.drift * Math.max(0, 1 - delta * 3); D.impact = Math.max(0, D.impact - delta * 3); }; // ── Kameralar ────────────────────────────────────────────── const CAMS = ['Chase', 'Far chase', 'Hood', 'Cockpit', 'Bumper']; D.camNext = function () { D.camMode = (D.camMode + 1) % CAMS.length; HZ.ui.toast && HZ.ui.toast('Camera: ' + CAMS[D.camMode]); }; const _v = new THREE.Vector3(), _q = new THREE.Quaternion(), _e = new THREE.Euler(), _t = new THREE.Vector3(); D.camYaw = null; D.camPos = new THREE.Vector3(); D.camera = function (delta) { const car = HZ.player, ud = car.userData, sp = D.spec; if (!car || !sp) return; if (HZ.photo && HZ.photo.active) return; const cam = camera, av = Math.abs(ud.speed), vk = clamp(av / Math.max(30, D.vmax), 0, 1); const interior = D.camMode >= 2; if (HZ.model) { HZ.model.traverse(o => { if (o.name === 'Glass' || o.name === 'Roof') o.visible = !(D.camMode === 3); }); const I = HZ.model.userData.interior; if (I) { I.visible = D.camMode === 3; const sw = I.getObjectByName('SteeringWheel'); if (sw) sw.rotation.z = -(car.userData.steer || 0) * 1.6; } } if (D.camYaw === null) D.camYaw = car.rotation.y; // yo'nalish: mashina yaw + harakat yo'nalishiga qisman let yawT = car.rotation.y; const lat = ud.lateralVel || 0; if (av > 2) yawT -= clamp(Math.atan2(lat, Math.max(av, 1)), -0.5, 0.5) * 0.7; let dy = yawT - D.camYaw; dy = Math.atan2(Math.sin(dy), Math.cos(dy)); D.camYaw += dy * (1 - Math.exp(-delta * (interior ? 40 : 4.2))); const sy = D.camYaw; const px = car.position.x, py = car.position.y, pz = car.position.z; let fov = D.fovBase + 20 * Math.pow(vk, 1.5) + (HZ.nitroOn ? 6 : 0); if (!D.dragging) { D.idleT += delta; if (D.idleT > 2.2) { const k = 1 - Math.exp(-delta * 2.6); D.lookYaw -= D.lookYaw * k; D.lookPitch -= D.lookPitch * k; } } else { D.lookYaw = Math.atan2(Math.sin(D.lookYaw), Math.cos(D.lookYaw)); } if (!interior) { const far = D.camMode === 1, zoom = D.camZoom || 1; const dist0 = ((far ? 9.4 : 6.6) + vk * 1.4 + sp.L * 0.14) * zoom, hgt0 = (far ? 3.6 : 2.35) + (sp.hp.mass > 2000 ? 0.45 : 0); const cyy = py + 1.0, ly = D.lookYaw, phi = clamp(Math.atan2(hgt0 - 1.0, dist0) + D.lookPitch, -0.25, 1.45), rr = Math.hypot(dist0, hgt0 - 1.0), yaw = sy + ly, cp = Math.cos(phi); _t.set(px + Math.sin(yaw) * cp * rr, cyy + Math.sin(phi) * rr, pz + Math.cos(yaw) * cp * rr); const k = 1 - Math.exp(-delta * (D.dragging || Math.abs(ly) > 0.01 ? 16 : 9)); D.camPos.lerp(_t, D.camPos.lengthSq() === 0 ? 1 : k); const gh = W.groundAt(D.camPos.x, D.camPos.z) + 0.6; if (D.camPos.y < gh) D.camPos.y = gh; cam.position.copy(D.camPos); cam.position.y += Math.sin(HZ.t * 38) * 0.012 * vk * (D.surf > 0 ? 3 : 1); const ahead = clamp(1 - Math.abs(ly) / 0.6, 0, 1), sinY = Math.sin(sy), cosY = Math.cos(sy); _v.set(px - sinY * (4 + vk * 6) * ahead, py + 1.0 + (far ? 0.6 : 0.3) * ahead, pz - cosY * (4 + vk * 6) * ahead); cam.up.set(0, 1, 0); cam.lookAt(_v); } else { D.camPos.set(0, 0, 0); const off = D.camMode === 2 ? [0, 0.64 + sp.gc * 0, -sp.L * 0.12] : D.camMode === 3 ? [-0.0, 0.85 + (sp.hp.mass > 2000 ? 0.5 : 0), sp.L * 0.04] : [0, 0.12, -sp.L * 0.5 - 0.05]; const topY = (sp.top[3] ? sp.top[3][1] : 0.9); if (D.camMode === 2) off[1] = topY + 0.32; if (D.camMode === 3) off[1] = (sp.cab ? sp.cab[1][1] : topY + 0.4) - 0.38 - 0.0; _v.set(off[0], off[1] - 0.6 + 0.0, off[2]); _v.applyQuaternion(car.quaternion); cam.position.set(px + _v.x, py + _v.y, pz + _v.z); cam.quaternion.copy(car.quaternion); cam.rotateY(clamp(D.lookYaw, -2.7, 2.7)); cam.rotateX(-clamp(D.lookPitch, -0.4, 0.9) * 0.8); fov += 6; // chap/o'ngga qarash (A/D bilan emas — Q/E) } // zarba/titrash if (D.impact > 0.01) { cam.position.x += (Math.random() - 0.5) * D.impact * 0.35; cam.position.y += (Math.random() - 0.5) * D.impact * 0.35; } cam.fov += (fov - cam.fov) * Math.min(1, delta * 4); cam.near = 0.25; cam.far = 3600; cam.updateProjectionMatrix(); }; // Garaj kamerasi: mashina atrofida orbita D.garageCam = function (delta) { const car = HZ.player; D.orbit = (D.orbit || 0) + (D.dragging ? 0 : delta * 0.35) + D.orbitDrag; D.orbitDrag = 0; const r = 7.2 + D.spec.L * 0.25, cam = camera; cam.position.set(car.position.x + Math.sin(D.orbit) * r, car.position.y + 1.6, car.position.z + Math.cos(D.orbit) * r); const rgx = Math.cos(D.orbit), rgz = -Math.sin(D.orbit); cam.lookAt(car.position.x - rgx * 2.4, car.position.y + 0.3, car.position.z - rgz * 2.4); cam.fov = 45; cam.updateProjectionMatrix(); if (HZ.model && HZ.model.userData.interior) HZ.model.userData.interior.visible = false; if (HZ.model) HZ.model.traverse(o => { if (o.name === 'Glass' || o.name === 'Roof') o.visible = true; }); }; // ── Qayta tiklash ────────────────────────────────────────── D.placeAt = function (x, z, ry, y) { const car = HZ.player; if (!car) return; car.position.set(x, (y !== undefined ? y : W.groundAt(x, z)) + 0.62, z); car.rotation.set(0, ry, 0, 'YXZ'); car.userData.speed = 0; car.userData.lateralVel = 0; car.userData.steer = 0; D.camYaw = ry; D.camPos.set(0, 0, 0); D.pending = true; }; // eng yaqin yo'lga (yo'nalish bo'ylab) qaytarish D.toRoad = function () { const car = HZ.player; W.nearestRoad(car.position.x, car.position.z, 260); const Q = W.Q; if (!Q.found) return false; const R = W.roads[Q.road], i = Q.i; let ry = Math.atan2(-R.tx[i], -R.tz[i]); // old = −Z → yaw: forward vec (−sin ry, −cos ry) // yo'l bo'ylab hozirgi yo'nalishga yaqinini tanlash const cur = car.rotation.y, a = ry, b = ry + Math.PI; const dd = (p, q) => Math.abs(Math.atan2(Math.sin(p - q), Math.cos(p - q))); const use = dd(a, cur) <= dd(b, cur) ? a : b; const lane = R.hw[i] * 0.5 * (use === a ? 1 : -1) * 0.0; D.placeAt(R.x[i] - R.tz[i] * lane, R.z[i] + R.tx[i] * lane, use, R.y[i]); return true; }; })(); // ============================================================ // APEX HORIZON — r5_audio.js // sounds/ papkasidagi fayllar (wav/ogg/mp3) bo'lsa — ular; bo'lmasa WebAudio sintezi. // engine/.wav yoki engine/engine.wav (aylanuvchi sikl, tezligi RPM ga qarab o'zgaradi) // ambient/{wind,rain,roadnoise} (sikl) ambient/thunder // car/{skid,turbo} (sikl) car/{impact,shift,horn} ui/{beep,go,reward,click} // ============================================================ (function () { const D = HZ.drive, S = HZ.state, clamp = HZ.W.clamp; const A = HZ.audio = { ctx: null, vol: 0.7, muted: false, ok: false, F: {}, FE: null, engKey: '' }; function noiseBuf(ctx) { const b = ctx.createBuffer(1, ctx.sampleRate * 2, ctx.sampleRate), d = b.getChannelData(0); for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) d[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1; return b; } A.start = function () { if (A.ctx) { if (A.ctx.state === 'suspended') A.ctx.resume(); return; } try { const ctx = A.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); const master = A.master = ctx.createGain(); master.gain.value = A.muted ? 0 : A.vol; master.connect(ctx.destination); const comp = ctx.createDynamicsCompressor(); comp.connect(master); A.out = comp; A.eng = { o: [], g: ctx.createGain(), f: ctx.createBiquadFilter() }; A.eng.f.type = 'lowpass'; A.eng.f.frequency.value = 900; A.eng.f.Q.value = 3; A.eng.g.gain.value = 0; for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = i === 0 ? 'sawtooth' : i === 1 ? 'square' : 'triangle'; const g = ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = [0.5, 0.18, 0.4][i]; o.connect(g); g.connect(A.eng.f); o.start(); A.eng.o.push(o); } A.eng.f.connect(A.eng.g); A.eng.g.connect(A.out); const nb = noiseBuf(ctx); const mkN = (type, f, q) => { const s = ctx.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = nb; s.loop = true; const fl = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); fl.type = type; fl.frequency.value = f; fl.Q.value = q; const g = ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = 0; s.connect(fl); fl.connect(g); g.connect(A.out); s.start(); return { s, fl, g }; }; A.wind = mkN('bandpass', 500, 0.6); A.tire = mkN('bandpass', 1800, 4); A.road = mkN('lowpass', 220, 0.8); A.rain = mkN('highpass', 2400, 0.5); A.turbo = mkN('bandpass', 4200, 8); A.ok = true; HZ.assets.decode(ctx).then(A.setupFiles); } catch (e) { console.warn('audio', e); } }; // fayldan sikl manbasi function loop(key) { const b = HZ.assets.sound(key); if (!b || !A.ctx) return null; const s = A.ctx.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = b; s.loop = true; const g = A.ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = 0; s.connect(g); g.connect(A.out); s.start(); return { s, g }; } A.setupFiles = function () { if (!A.ctx) return; for (const [k, key] of [['wind', 'ambient/wind'], ['rain', 'ambient/rain'], ['road', 'ambient/roadnoise'], ['skid', 'car/skid'], ['turbo', 'car/turbo']]) { if (!A.F[k]) A.F[k] = loop(key); } A.rebuildEngine(); }; A.rebuildEngine = function () { if (!A.ctx || !D.spec) return; const key = D.spec.id; if (A.engKey === key && A.FE) return; A.engKey = key; if (A.FE) { try { A.FE.s.stop(); } catch (e) {} A.FE = null; } const b = HZ.assets.engineBuf(key); if (!b) return; const s = A.ctx.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = b; s.loop = true; const g = A.ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = 0; s.connect(g); g.connect(A.out); s.start(); A.FE = { s, g }; }; HZ.listen('car', () => { A.engKey = ''; A.rebuildEngine(); }); A.stop = function () { try { if (A.ctx) A.ctx.close(); } catch (e) {} A.ctx = null; A.ok = false; A.F = {}; A.FE = null; A.engKey = ''; }; A.setVol = function (v) { A.vol = v; if (A.master) A.master.gain.value = A.muted ? 0 : v; }; A.mute = function (m) { A.muted = m; if (A.master) A.master.gain.value = m ? 0 : A.vol; }; A.blip = function (f, t, type, vol, slide) { if (!A.ok) return; const ctx = A.ctx, o = ctx.createOscillator(), g = ctx.createGain(); o.type = type || 'sine'; o.frequency.value = f; if (slide) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(20, f * slide), ctx.currentTime + t); g.gain.value = vol || 0.2; g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, ctx.currentTime + t); o.connect(g); g.connect(A.out); o.start(); o.stop(ctx.currentTime + t + 0.02); }; // bir martalik fayl ovozi; fayl bo'lmasa false A.play = function (key, vol, rate) { const b = HZ.assets.sound(key); if (!b || !A.ok) return false; const s = A.ctx.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = b; if (rate) s.playbackRate.value = rate; const g = A.ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = vol === undefined ? 1 : vol; s.connect(g); g.connect(A.out); s.start(); return true; }; A.thump = function (v) { if (!A.ok) return; if (A.play('car/impact', clamp(0.35 + v, 0.2, 1.2), 0.9 + Math.random() * 0.2)) return; }; }; this.onValue = (path, cb) => { const s = { kind: 'value', path: segs(path).join('/'), cb }; subs.push(s); setTimeout(() => cb(clone(get(s.path))), 0); return () => { const i = subs.indexOf(s); if (i >= 0) subs.splice(i, 1); }); } g.model = HZ.cars.make(sp, { shop: false }); g.model.userData.isGhost = true; HZ.root.add(g.model); g.carId = carId; g.spec = sp; if (data.car.color) HZ.cars.paintCar(g.model, data.car.color, data.car.finish); MP.tick = function (delta) { if (!HZ.net.on) return; const now = HZ.net.now(); MP.host = computeHost(); if (MP.isHost()) startHostDuty(); else stopHostDuty(); // 1) o'z holatini yuborish (throttled — cfg.tickHz, standart 10 Hz) const hz = (HZ.net.cfg && HZ.net.cfg.tickHz) || 10; if (now - MP.lastSend > 1000 / hz) { MP.lastSend = now; const c = HZ.player, ud = c.userData, sp = D.spec; HZ.net.sendState({ x: +c.position.x.toFixed(2), y: +c.position.y.toFixed(2), z: +c.position.z.toFixed(2), ry: +c.rotation.y.toFixed(3), kmh: Math.round(Math.abs(ud.speed || 0) * 3.6), car: sp ? sp.id : '', lights: HZ.lightsOn(), brake: !!(typeof fpsKeys !== 'undefined' && fpsKeys['KeyS']), horn: !!MP._horn, t: now }); MP._horn = false; // o'z mashinasi/pul meta'si (ismi, rangi) — faqat KO'RINISH uchun, pul balansiga bu yerdan tegilmaydi if (sp && (!HZ.net.meta.car || HZ.net.meta.car.id !== sp.id || HZ.net.meta.car.color !== sp.color)) HZ.net.setMeta({ car: { id: sp.id, color: sp.color, finish: sp.finish } }); } // 2) o'z balansini serverdan (host yozgan qiymatdan) kuzatish — birinchi marta o'qilganda joriy mahalliy qiymat yuboriladi if (!MP._credSynced) { MP._credSynced = true; HZ.net.A.update('players/' + HZ.net.uid, { credits: HZ.save.credits }); } // 3) ruhlar: pozitsiya, chiroq/signal, TEZLIK-HACK tekshiruvi for (const uid in MP.ghosts) { const g = MP.ghosts[uid], st = (HZ.net.players[uid] || {}).st; if (!g.model) continue; if (now - g.lastSeen > 8000) { HZ.root.remove(g.model); delete MP.ghosts[uid]; continue; } if (st) { _v.set(st.x, st.y, st.z); if (!g.inited) { g.model.position.copy(_v); g.model.rotation.y = st.ry; g.inited = true; } else { g.model.position.lerp(_v, Math.min(1, delta * 10)); let dr = st.ry - g.model.rotation.y; dr = Math.atan2(Math.sin(dr), Math.cos(dr)); g.model.rotation.y += dr * Math.min(1, delta * 10); } const ud2 = g.model.userData; if (ud2.hlMats) for (const m of ud2.hlMats) m.emissiveIntensity = st.lights ? 1.8 : 0.25; if (ud2.tlMats) for (const m of ud2.tlMats) m.emissiveIntensity = st.brake ? 2.4 : (st.lights ? 0.9 : 0.25); if (ud2.lights) for (const l of ud2.lights) l.intensity = st.lights ? 420 : 0; if (st.horn && st._hc !== g._hornSeen) { g._hornSeen = st._hc; HZ.audio && HZ.audio.horn && HZ.audio.horn(); } // ── TEZLIK-HACK: e'lon qilingan tezlik shu mashinaning pasport (vmax) dan +100 km/h dan oshsa — kick const sp = g.spec, vmax = sp ? sp.hp.vmax : 260, allowed = vmax + MP.SPEEDHACK_MARGIN; if (st.kmh > allowed) { MP.violStreak[uid] = (MP.violStreak[uid] || 0) + 1; } else MP.violStreak[uid] = 0; if (MP.violStreak[uid] >= 3) { // ≥3 ketma-ket o'lchov (≈0.3–0.6 s) — tarmoq sakrashidan himoya MP.kick(uid, 'Speed hack suspected (' + st.kmh + ' > ' + Math.round(allowed) + ' km/h)'); MP.violStreak[uid] = 0; } } } }; // ── Kick: o'yinchi tugunini o'chirish + qisqa muddatli "ban" belgisi (darhol qayta ulanib olmasin) MP.kick = function (uid, reason) { if (!HZ.net.on) return; HZ.ui && HZ.ui.toast && HZ.ui.toast('⛔ ' + ((HZ.net.players[uid] && HZ.net.players[uid].name) || uid) + ' kicked: ' + reason, ''); HZ.net.chat('⚠ ' + ((HZ.net.players[uid] && HZ.net.players[uid].name) || 'Player') + ' kicked — ' + reason); HZ.net.A.set('banned/' + uid, { t: HZ.net.now(), reason }); HZ.net.A.remove('players/' + uid); if (uid === HZ.net.uid) { HZ.ui.toast('You were kicked: ' + reason); HZ.net.disconnect(); } }; // Ulanishda: agar o'zim yaqinda kick qilingan bo'lsam — qayta ulanish rad etiladi (60 s sovutish) HZ.net.listen && HZ.net.listen('kickcheck', () => {}); const _origConnect = HZ.net.connect; HZ.net.connect = async function (profile) { const cfg = HZ.net.cfg || await HZ.net.loadConfig(); const uid = await _origConnect.call(HZ.net, profile); try { const ban = await new Promise(res => { const off = HZ.net.A.onValue('banned/' + uid, v => { off(); res(v); }); setTimeout(() => res(null), 400); }); if (ban && HZ.net.now() - ban.t < 60000) { HZ.net.disconnect(); throw new Error('You are temporarily banned (speed hack): wait ' + Math.ceil((60000 - (HZ.net.now() - ban.t)) / 1000) + 's'); } } catch (e) { if (/banned/.test(e.message)) throw e; } return uid; }; let credWatch = null, presenceWatch = null; HZ.net.listen('connected', () => { HZ.net._selfRemoving = false; if (credWatch) credWatch(); credWatch = HZ.net.A.onValue('players/' + HZ.net.uid + '/credits', (v) => { if (v == null) return; if (v !== HZ.save.credits) { HZ.save.credits = v; HZ.ui && HZ.ui.update && HZ.ui.update(0); } }); // ── O'z "mavjudlik" tugunini kuzatish: boshqa klient meni kick qilsa (players/{uid} ni o'chirsa), // men buni darhol sezib, mahalliy sessiyani to'xtataman (aks holda "zombi" holatda qolib ketaman). if (presenceWatch) presenceWatch(); let firstVal = true; presenceWatch = HZ.net.A.onValue('players/' + HZ.net.uid, (v) => { if (firstVal) { firstVal = false; return; } // obuna bo'lgandagi dastlabki qiymat — e'tiborsiz if (v == null && HZ.net.on && !HZ.net._selfRemoving) selfKicked(); }); }); function selfKicked() { const uid = HZ.net.uid; let off = null; off = HZ.net.A.onValue('banned/' + uid, (b) => { if (off) off(); const reason = (b && b.reason) || 'removed by host'; HZ.net._selfRemoving = true; HZ.net.disconnect(); HZ.ui && HZ.ui.toast && HZ.ui.toast('⛔ You were kicked: ' + reason); }); } HZ.net.listen('disconnect', () => { stopHostDuty(); if (credWatch) { credWatch(); credWatch = null; } if (presenceWatch) { presenceWatch(); presenceWatch = null; } }); })(); })(); } if (window.HZ && window.HZ.tick) window.HZ.tick(delta, time, self);
